14: //! Transcript gadget module, for in-circuit Fiat-Shamir.
15: //! Shall we adopt the [SAFE API](https://hackmd.io/bHgsH6mMStCVibM_wYvb2w)?

17: use ff::Field;
18: use midnight_proofs::{
19:     circuit::{Layouter, Value},
20:     plonk::Error,
21:     transcript::{CircuitTranscript, Transcript},
22: };

25:     instructions::{AssignmentInstructions, PublicInputInstructions, SpongeInstructions},

30: type SpongeState<S> = <<S as SelfEmulation>::SpongeChip as SpongeInstructions<
31:     <S as SelfEmulation>::F,
32:     AssignedNative<<S as SelfEmulation>::F>,
33:     AssignedNative<<S as SelfEmulation>::F>,
34: >>::State;
35: 
36: /// Gadget used to run the transcript reader in-circuit.
37: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
38: pub struct TranscriptGadget<S: SelfEmulation> {
39:     scalar_chip: S::ScalarChip,
40:     curve_chip: S::CurveChip,
41:     sponge_chip: S::SpongeChip,
42:     sponge_state: Option<SpongeState<S>>,
43:     // Track the number of field elements we have in the buffer.
44:     input_len: usize,
45:     // Transcript reader is included, to help parse the proof. This parsing
46:     // *does not* need to be verified in-circuit.
47:     transcript_reader: Option<CircuitTranscript<S::Hash>>,
48: }
49: 
50: impl<S: SelfEmulation> TranscriptGadget<S> {
51:     /// Creates a new `TranscriptGadget` from the corresponding chips.
52:     pub fn new(
53:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
54:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
55:         sponge_chip: &S::SpongeChip,
56:     ) -> Self {
57:         Self {
58:             scalar_chip: scalar_chip.clone(),
59:             curve_chip: curve_chip.clone(),
60:             sponge_chip: sponge_chip.clone(),
61:             sponge_state: None,
62:             input_len: 0,
63:             transcript_reader: None,
64:         }
65:     }
66: 
67:     /// Initialises the `TranscriptGadget`, by initialising the sponge buffer,
68:     /// from a given witnessed proof in the form of `Value<Vec<u8>>`.
69:     pub fn init_with_proof(
70:         &mut self,
71:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
72:         proof: Value<Vec<u8>>,
73:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
74:         self.sponge_state = Some(self.sponge_chip.init(layouter, None)?);
75: 
76:         // Unwrapping the witness. The amount of points read from the proof is
77:         // fixed for a given `Architecture`, and does not depend on the size of
78:         // the proof.
79:         // The caveat with this approach is that our in-circuit verifier will not
80:         // be able to verify that the proof did not include extra bytes after
81:         // all the relevant bytes have been read. This is not an issue anyway.
82:         let mut proof_bytes = Vec::new();
83:         proof.clone().map(|pi| proof_bytes.extend_from_slice(&pi));
84:         self.transcript_reader = Some(CircuitTranscript::init_from_bytes(&proof_bytes));
85: 
86:         Ok(())
87:     }
88: 
89:     /// Absorbs a scalar into the transcript.
90:     pub fn common_scalar(
91:         &mut self,
92:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
93:         scalar: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
94:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
95:         self.input_len += 1;
96:         let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
97:         self.sponge_chip.absorb(layouter, state, std::slice::from_ref(scalar))
98:     }
99: 
100:     /// Absorbs a point into the transcript.
101:     pub fn common_point(
102:         &mut self,
103:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
104:         point: &S::AssignedPoint,
105:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
106:         let pis = self.curve_chip.as_public_input(layouter, point)?;
107: 
108:         self.input_len += pis.len();
109: 
110:         let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
111:         self.sponge_chip.absorb(layouter, state, &pis)
112:     }
113: 
114:     /// Derives a scalar challenge from the current transcript.
115:     pub fn squeeze_challenge(
116:         &mut self,
117:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
118:     ) -> Result<AssignedNative<S::F>, Error> {
119:         let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
120:         self.sponge_chip.squeeze(layouter, state)
121:     }
122: 
123:     /// Reads a point from the reader buffer, and adds it to the transcript.
124:     /// Think of the read point as a witness freely chosen by the prover.
125:     ///
126:     /// # Warning
127:     ///
128:     /// The received points are not enforced to be part of the relevant prime
129:     /// order subgroup.
130:     pub fn read_point(
131:         &mut self,
132:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
133:     ) -> Result<S::AssignedPoint, Error> {
134:         let reader = self.transcript_reader.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
135:         // If an error, do not fail, assign a default point instead.
136:         // (This allows us to parse dummy proofs.)
137:         let point: Value<S::C> = match reader.read::<S::C>() {
138:             Ok(point) => Value::known(point),
139:             Err(_) => Value::known(S::C::default()),
140:         };
141: 
142:         let assigned_point = S::assign_without_subgroup_check(layouter, &self.curve_chip, point)?;
143:         self.common_point(layouter, &assigned_point)?;
144: 
145:         Ok(assigned_point)
146:     }
147: 
148:     /// Reads a scalar from the reader buffer, and adds it to the transcript.
149:     /// Think of the read scalar as a witness freely chosen by the prover.
150:     pub fn read_scalar(
151:         &mut self,
152:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
153:     ) -> Result<AssignedNative<S::F>, Error> {
154:         let reader = self.transcript_reader.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
155:         // If an error, do not fail, assign a default scalar instead.
156:         // (This allows us to parse dummy proofs.)
157:         let scalar: Value<S::F> = match reader.read::<S::F>() {
158:             Ok(scalar) => Value::known(scalar),
159:             Err(_) => Value::known(S::F::ZERO),
160:         };
161: 
162:         let assigned_scalar = self.scalar_chip.assign(layouter, scalar)?;
163:         self.common_scalar(layouter, &assigned_scalar)?;
164: 
165:         Ok(assigned_scalar)
166:     }
167: }

189:         let scalar_chip = S::ScalarChip::new_from_scratch(&config.0);
190:         let curve_chip = S::CurveChip::new_from_scratch(&config.1);
191:         let sponge_chip = S::SpongeChip::new_from_scratch(&config.2);
192:         TranscriptGadget::new(&scalar_chip, &curve_chip, &sponge_chip)

196:         self.scalar_chip.load_from_scratch(layouter)?;
197:         self.curve_chip.load_from_scratch(layouter)?;
198:         self.sponge_chip.load_from_scratch(layouter)
199:     }
200:

204:         fixed_columns: &mut Vec<Column<Fixed>>,
205:         instance_columns: &[Column<Instance>; 2],
206:     ) -> Self::Config {
207:         (
208:             S::ScalarChip::configure_from_scratch(
209:                 meta,
210:                 advice_columns,
211:                 fixed_columns,
212:                 instance_columns,
213:             ),
214:             S::CurveChip::configure_from_scratch(
215:                 meta,
216:                 advice_columns,
217:                 fixed_columns,
218:                 instance_columns,
219:             ),
220:             S::SpongeChip::configure_from_scratch(
221:                 meta,
222:                 advice_columns,
223:                 fixed_columns,
224:                 instance_columns,
225:             ),
226:         )
227:     }

245:     const SIZE: usize = 12;
246: 
247:     type S = BlstrsEmulation;
248: 
249:     type F = <S as SelfEmulation>::F;
250:     type C = <S as SelfEmulation>::C;
251: 
252:     #[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
253:     struct TestCircuit {
254:         points: Value<[C; SIZE]>,
255:         scalars: Value<[F; SIZE]>,
256:     }
257: 
258:     fn configure(
259:         meta: &mut ConstraintSystem<F>,
260:     ) -> <TranscriptGadget<S> as FromScratch<F>>::Config {
261:         let committed_instance_column = meta.instance_column();
262:         let instance_column = meta.instance_column();
263:         TranscriptGadget::<S>::configure_from_scratch(
264:             meta,
265:             &mut vec![],
266:             &mut vec![],
267:             &[committed_instance_column, instance_column],
268:         )
269:     }
270: 
271:     impl Circuit<F> for TestCircuit {
272:         type Config = <TranscriptGadget<S> as FromScratch<F>>::Config;
273:         type FloorPlanner = SimpleFloorPlanner;
274:         type Params = ();
275: 
276:         fn without_witnesses(&self) -> Self {
277:             TestCircuit::default()
278:         }
279: 
280:         fn configure(meta: &mut ConstraintSystem<F>) -> Self::Config {
281:             configure(meta)
282:         }
283: 
284:         fn synthesize(
285:             &self,
286:             config: Self::Config,
287:             mut layouter: impl Layouter<F>,
288:         ) -> Result<(), Error> {
289:             let mut transcript_gadget = TranscriptGadget::<S>::new_from_scratch(&config);
290:             transcript_gadget.init_with_proof(&mut layouter, Value::unknown())?;
291: 
292:             let assigned_scalars = transcript_gadget
293:                 .scalar_chip
294:                 .assign_many(&mut layouter, &self.scalars.transpose_array())?;
295: 
296:             let assigned_points = self
297:                 .points
298:                 .transpose_array()
299:                 .iter()
300:                 .map(|p| {
301:                     S::assign_without_subgroup_check(
302:                         &mut layouter,
303:                         &transcript_gadget.curve_chip,
304:                         *p,
305:                     )
306:                 })
307:                 .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
308: 
309:             for i in 0..(SIZE / 2) {
310:                 transcript_gadget.common_scalar(&mut layouter, &assigned_scalars[i])?;
311:                 transcript_gadget.common_point(&mut layouter, &assigned_points[i])?;
312:             }
313: 
314:             let challenge_1 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(&mut layouter)?;
315:             transcript_gadget
316:                 .scalar_chip
317:                 .constrain_as_public_input(&mut layouter, &challenge_1)?;
318: 
319:             for i in (SIZE / 2)..SIZE {
320:                 transcript_gadget.common_scalar(&mut layouter, &assigned_scalars[i])?;
321:                 transcript_gadget.common_point(&mut layouter, &assigned_points[i])?;
322:             }
323: 
324:             let challenge_2 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(&mut layouter)?;
325:             transcript_gadget
326:                 .scalar_chip
327:                 .constrain_as_public_input(&mut layouter, &challenge_2)?;
328: 
329:             transcript_gadget.load_from_scratch(&mut layouter)
330:         }
331:     }
332:

336:         let points: [C; SIZE] = core::array::from_fn(|_| C::random(OsRng));
337:

341:         };

344:

346:             off_circuit_transcript.common(&scalars[i]).unwrap();
347:             off_circuit_transcript.common::<C>(&points[i]).unwrap();
348:         }
349: 
350:         let challenge_1: F = off_circuit_transcript.squeeze_challenge();
351: 
352:         for i in (SIZE / 2)..SIZE {
353:             off_circuit_transcript.common(&scalars[i]).unwrap();
354:             off_circuit_transcript.common::<C>(&points[i]).unwrap();
355:         }
356:

358: 
359:         let public_inputs = vec![vec![], vec![challenge_1, challenge_2]];

14: //! A module for in-circuit trash arguments. It is the in-circuit analog
15: //! of file proofs/src/plonk/lookup/verifier.rs.
16: //!
17: //! The "expressions" part is dealt with in our `expressions/` directory.
18: 
19: use midnight_proofs::{circuit::Layouter, plonk::Error, poly::CommitmentLabel};
20: 
21: use crate::{
22:     field::AssignedNative,
23:     verifier::{
24:         kzg::VerifierQuery, transcript_gadget::TranscriptGadget, utils::AssignedBoundedScalar,
25:         SelfEmulation,
26:     },
27: };
28: 
29: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
30: pub(crate) struct TrashEvaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
31:     pub(crate) trash_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
32: }
33: 
34: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
35: pub(crate) struct Committed<S: SelfEmulation> {
36:     trash_commitment: S::AssignedPoint,
37: }
38: 
39: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
40: pub(crate) struct Evaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
41:     committed: Committed<S>,
42:     pub(crate) evaluated: TrashEvaluated<S>,
43: }
44: 
45: pub(crate) fn read_committed<S: SelfEmulation>(
46:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
47:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
48: ) -> Result<Committed<S>, Error> {
49:     let trash_commitment = transcript_gadget.read_point(layouter)?;
50: 
51:     Ok(Committed { trash_commitment })
52: }
53: 
54: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Committed<S> {
55:     pub(crate) fn evaluate(
56:         self,
57:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
58:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
59:     ) -> Result<Evaluated<S>, Error> {
60:         let trash_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
61: 
62:         Ok(Evaluated {
63:             committed: self,
64:             evaluated: TrashEvaluated { trash_eval },
65:         })
66:     }
67: }
68: 
69: // "expressions" is implemented in `expressions/trash.rs`
70: 
71: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Evaluated<S> {
72:     pub(crate) fn queries(
73:         &self,
74:         one: &AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>, // 1
75:         x: &AssignedNative<S::F>,          // evaluation point x
76:     ) -> Vec<VerifierQuery<S>> {
77:         vec![VerifierQuery::new(
78:             one,
79:             x,
80:             CommitmentLabel::NoLabel,
81:             &self.committed.trash_commitment,
82:             &self.evaluated.trash_eval,
83:         )]
84:     }
85: }

14: //! Module that contains type and generic bounds.
15: //! Its purpose is to minimize complexity in the rest of the verifier chip.
16: 
17: use std::fmt::Debug;
18: 
19: use ff::WithSmallOrderMulGroup;
20: use group::{prime::PrimeCurveAffine, Curve};
21: use midnight_curves::{
22:     pairing::{Engine, MultiMillerLoop},
23:     serde::SerdeObject,
24:     CurveAffine, CurveExt,
25: };
26: use midnight_proofs::{
27:     circuit::{Layouter, Value},
28:     plonk::Error,
29:     transcript::{Hashable, TranscriptHash},
30: };

32: #[cfg(not(feature = "truncated-challenges"))]
33: use crate::instructions::FieldInstructions;
34: #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
35: use crate::instructions::NativeInstructions;
36: use crate::{
37:     ecc::{
38:         curves::{CircuitCurve, WeierstrassCurve},
39:         foreign::weierstrass_chip::ForeignWeierstrassEccChip,
40:     },

45:         HashInstructions, PublicInputInstructions, SpongeInstructions,
46:     },
47:     types::{AssignedForeignPoint, InnerValue, Instantiable},
48:     CircuitField,

51: /// A trait for parametrizing the VerifierGadget.
52: pub trait SelfEmulation: Clone + Debug {
53:     /// The native field.
54:     type F: CircuitField + WithSmallOrderMulGroup<3> + Hashable<Self::Hash>;
55: 
56:     /// The underlying curve of the self-emulation proof.
57:     type C: CurveExt<ScalarExt = Self::F, AffineExt = Self::G1Affine>
58:         + WeierstrassCurve<CryptographicGroup = Self::C, Base = <Self::C as CurveExt>::Base>
59:         + Hashable<Self::Hash>;
60: 
61:     /// An assigned point of curve C.
62:     type AssignedPoint: InnerValue<Element = Self::C> + Instantiable<Self::F> + PartialEq + Eq;
63: 
64:     /// A type for the Fiat-Shamir hashing.
65:     type Hash: TranscriptHash;
66: 
67:     #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
68:     /// A chip implementing native field arithmetic operations.
69:     type ScalarChip: NativeInstructions<Self::F>;
70:     #[cfg(not(feature = "truncated-challenges"))]
71:     /// A chip implementing native field arithmetic operations.
72:     type ScalarChip: FieldInstructions<Self::F, AssignedNative<Self::F>>;
73: 
74:     /// A chip implementing assignment operations for [Self::AssignedPoint].
75:     type CurveChip: Clone
76:         + AssignmentInstructions<Self::F, Self::AssignedPoint>
77:         + PublicInputInstructions<Self::F, Self::AssignedPoint>;
78: 
79:     /// A chip implementing sponge operations over the native field.
80:     type SpongeChip: Clone
81:         + SpongeInstructions<Self::F, AssignedNative<Self::F>, AssignedNative<Self::F>>
82:         + HashInstructions<Self::F, AssignedNative<Self::F>, AssignedNative<Self::F>>;
83: 
84:     /// C in affine form (first source group).
85:     type G1Affine: CurveAffine<ScalarExt = Self::F, CurveExt = Self::C, Base = <Self::C as CircuitCurve>::Base>
86:         + Into<Self::C>
87:         + From<Self::C>
88:         + SerdeObject;
89: 
90:     /// The second source group.
91:     type G2Affine: PrimeCurveAffine + From<<Self::Engine as Engine>::G2> + SerdeObject;
92: 
93:     /// Wrapper type for the pairing engine.
94:     type Engine: Engine
95:         + MultiMillerLoop<
96:             Fr = Self::F,
97:             G1 = Self::C,
98:             G1Affine = <Self::C as Curve>::AffineRepr,
99:             G2Affine = Self::G2Affine,
100:         >;
101: 
102:     /// Variable-base multi-scalar multiplication, the `usize` next to each
103:     /// scalar is an (inclusive) upper-bound on their bit-length.
104:     ///
105:     /// # Panics
106:     ///
107:     /// If `|scalars| != |bases|`.
108:     fn msm(
109:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
110:         curve_chip: &Self::CurveChip,
111:         scalars: &[(AssignedNative<Self::F>, usize)],
112:         bases: &[Self::AssignedPoint],
113:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedPoint, Error>;
114: 
115:     /// Constrains the given scalar as a committed public input.
116:     fn constrain_scalar_as_committed_public_input(
117:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
118:         scalar_chip: &Self::ScalarChip,
119:         assigned_scalar: &AssignedNative<Self::F>,
120:     ) -> Result<(), Error>;
121: 
122:     /// Assigns a point without checking that it is part of the prime order
123:     /// subgroup.
124:     /// Allowing points outside the subgroup does not give any advantage to an
125:     /// adversary in PLONK.
126:     // TODO: Provide a formal analysis of subgroup soundness in this context.
127:     fn assign_without_subgroup_check(
128:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
129:         curve_chip: &Self::CurveChip,
130:         base: Value<Self::C>,
131:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedPoint, Error>;
132: }
133: 
134: // Implementations
135: 
136: /// Implementation of the SelfEmulation trait for blstrs.
137: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
138: pub struct BlstrsEmulation {}
139: 
140: impl SelfEmulation for BlstrsEmulation {
141:     type F = midnight_curves::Fq;
142:     type C = midnight_curves::G1Projective;
143:     type AssignedPoint = AssignedForeignPoint<Self::F, Self::C, Self::C>;
144:     type Hash = PoseidonState<Self::F>;
145: 
146:     type ScalarChip = NativeGadget<Self::F, P2RDecompositionChip<Self::F>, NativeChip<Self::F>>;
147:     type CurveChip =
148:         ForeignWeierstrassEccChip<Self::F, Self::C, Self::C, Self::ScalarChip, Self::ScalarChip>;
149:     type SpongeChip = PoseidonChip<Self::F>;
150: 
151:     type G1Affine = midnight_curves::G1Affine;
152:     type G2Affine = midnight_curves::G2Affine;
153:     type Engine = midnight_curves::Bls12;
154: 
155:     fn msm(
156:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
157:         curve_chip: &Self::CurveChip,
158:         scalars: &[(AssignedNative<Self::F>, usize)],
159:         bases: &[Self::AssignedPoint],
160:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedPoint, Error> {
161:         curve_chip.msm_by_bounded_scalars(layouter, scalars, bases)
162:     }
163: 
164:     fn constrain_scalar_as_committed_public_input(
165:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
166:         scalar_chip: &Self::ScalarChip,
167:         assigned_scalar: &AssignedNative<Self::F>,
168:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
169:         scalar_chip.constrain_as_committed_public_input(layouter, assigned_scalar)
170:     }
171: 
172:     fn assign_without_subgroup_check(
173:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<Self::F>,
174:         curve_chip: &Self::CurveChip,
175:         base: Value<Self::C>,
176:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedPoint, Error> {
177:         curve_chip.assign_without_subgroup_check(layouter, base)
178:     }
179: }

=== PRIMARY deliverables/sp04-native-finalizer-source-2026-09-10/point-origin-source-01/sources/midnight-circuits-7.2.4/src/verifier/vanishing.rs FULL FILE SHA256c427b7d5f1394e8f86e54ec75d39b14a4c0e2451fc81db413568f698947af697; FULL ===

14: //! A module for the in-circuit vanishing argument. This is the in-circuit
15: //! analog of file proofs/src/plonk/vanishing/verifier.rs.
16: 
17: use ff::Field;
18: use midnight_proofs::{circuit::Layouter, plonk::Error, poly::CommitmentLabel};
19: 
20: use crate::{
21:     field::AssignedNative,
22:     instructions::ArithInstructions,
23:     verifier::{
24:         kzg::VerifierQuery,
25:         msm::AssignedMsm,
26:         transcript_gadget::TranscriptGadget,
27:         utils::{mul_add, mul_bounded_scalars, try_reduce, AssignedBoundedScalar},
28:         SelfEmulation,
29:     },
30: };
31: 
32: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
33: pub(crate) struct Committed<S: SelfEmulation> {
34:     random_poly_commitment: S::AssignedPoint,
35: }
36: 
37: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
38: pub(crate) struct Constructed<S: SelfEmulation> {
39:     h_commitments: Vec<S::AssignedPoint>,
40:     random_poly_commitment: S::AssignedPoint,
41: }
42: 
43: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
44: pub(crate) struct PartiallyEvaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
45:     h_commitments: Vec<S::AssignedPoint>,
46:     random_poly_commitment: S::AssignedPoint,
47:     random_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
48: }
49: 
50: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
51: pub(crate) struct Evaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
52:     h_commitment: AssignedMsm<S>,
53:     random_poly_commitment: S::AssignedPoint,
54:     expected_h_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
55:     random_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
56: }
57: 
58: pub(crate) fn read_commitments_before_y<S: SelfEmulation>(
59:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
60:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
61: ) -> Result<Committed<S>, Error> {
62:     let random_poly_commitment = transcript_gadget.read_point(layouter)?;
63: 
64:     Ok(Committed {
65:         random_poly_commitment,
66:     })
67: }
68: 
69: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Committed<S> {
70:     pub(crate) fn read_commitment_after_y(
71:         self,
72:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
73:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
74:         quotient_poly_degree: usize,
75:     ) -> Result<Constructed<S>, Error> {
76:         let h_commitments = (0..quotient_poly_degree)
77:             .map(|_| transcript_gadget.read_point(layouter))
78:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
79: 
80:         Ok(Constructed {
81:             h_commitments,
82:             random_poly_commitment: self.random_poly_commitment,
83:         })
84:     }
85: }
86: 
87: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Constructed<S> {
88:     pub(crate) fn evaluate_after_x(
89:         self,
90:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
91:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
92:     ) -> Result<PartiallyEvaluated<S>, Error> {
93:         let random_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
94: 
95:         Ok(PartiallyEvaluated {
96:             h_commitments: self.h_commitments,
97:             random_poly_commitment: self.random_poly_commitment,
98:             random_eval,
99:         })
100:     }
101: }
102: 
103: impl<S: SelfEmulation> PartiallyEvaluated<S> {
104:     pub(crate) fn verify(
105:         &self,
106:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
107:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
108:         expressions: &[AssignedNative<S::F>],
109:         y: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
110:         xn: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
111:         splitting_factor: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
112:     ) -> Result<Evaluated<S>, Error> {
113:         let expected_h_eval = {
114:             let num = try_reduce(expressions.iter().cloned(), |h_eval, v| {
115:                 // h_eval * y + v
116:                 mul_add(layouter, scalar_chip, &h_eval, y, &v)
117:             })?;
118:             let den = scalar_chip.add_constant(layouter, xn, -S::F::ONE)?;
119:             scalar_chip.div(layouter, &num, &den)?
120:         };
121: 
122:         let splitting_factor = AssignedBoundedScalar::new(splitting_factor, None);
123:         let mut acc = AssignedBoundedScalar::one(layouter, scalar_chip)?;
124: 
125:         let mut h_commitment = AssignedMsm::from_term(&acc, &self.h_commitments[0]);
126:         for h_com in self.h_commitments.iter().skip(1) {
127:             acc = mul_bounded_scalars(layouter, scalar_chip, &acc, &splitting_factor)?;
128:             h_commitment.add_term(&acc, h_com);
129:         }
130: 
131:         Ok(Evaluated {
132:             h_commitment,
133:             random_poly_commitment: self.random_poly_commitment.clone(),
134:             expected_h_eval,
135:             random_eval: self.random_eval.clone(),
136:         })
137:     }
138: }
139: 
140: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Evaluated<S> {
141:     pub(crate) fn queries(
142:         &self,
143:         one: &AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>, // 1
144:         x: &AssignedNative<S::F>,          // evaluation point x
145:     ) -> Vec<VerifierQuery<S>> {
146:         vec![
147:             VerifierQuery::new_from_msm(
148:                 x,
149:                 CommitmentLabel::Custom("vanishing".into()),
150:                 &self.h_commitment,
151:                 &self.expected_h_eval,
152:             ),
153:             VerifierQuery::new(
154:                 one,
155:                 x,
156:                 CommitmentLabel::Custom("random_poly".into()),
157:                 &self.random_poly_commitment,
158:                 &self.random_eval,
159:             ),
160:         ]
161:     }
162: }

124: impl<S: SelfEmulation> PublicInputInstructions<S::F, AssignedAccumulator<S>> for VerifierGadget<S> {

126:         &self,
127:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
128:         assigned: &AssignedAccumulator<S>,
129:     ) -> Result<Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>, Error> {
130:         Ok([

133:         ]

138:         &self,

140:         assigned: &AssignedAccumulator<S>,

142:         (assigned.lhs).constrain_as_public_input(layouter, &self.curve_chip, &self.scalar_chip)?;
143:         (assigned.rhs).constrain_as_public_input(layouter, &self.curve_chip, &self.scalar_chip)
144:     }

147:         &self,
148:         _layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
149:         _value: Value<Accumulator<S>>,

151:         unimplemented!(

153:         )

157: impl<S: SelfEmulation> VerifierGadget<S> {
158:     /// Constrains the given accumulator as a public input. The (fixed and
159:     /// non-fixed) scalars of its RHS are constrained in committed form (as a
160:     /// committed instance), whereas the rest of the accumulator is constrained
161:     /// as a normal instance.
162:     ///
163:     /// See [AssignedAccumulator::as_public_input_with_committed_scalars] for
164:     /// the off-circuit analog of this function.
165:     pub fn constrain_acc_as_public_input_with_committed_scalars(
166:         &self,
167:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
168:         acc: &AssignedAccumulator<S>,
169:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
170:         (acc.lhs).constrain_as_public_input(layouter, &self.curve_chip, &self.scalar_chip)?;
171:         (acc.rhs).constrain_as_public_input_with_committed_scalars(
172:             layouter,
173:             &self.curve_chip,
174:             &self.scalar_chip,
175:         )
176:     }
177: 
178:     /// Witnesses an accumulator with just 1 non-fixed base-scalar pair on each
179:     /// side (the scalar being constant 1), and as many fixed-base scalars (on
180:     /// its right-hand-side) as provided through the `fixed_base_names`
181:     /// argument (no fixed-base scalars on the left-hand-side).
182:     pub fn assign_collapsed_accumulator(
183:         &self,
184:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
185:         fixed_base_names: &[String],
186:         value: Value<Accumulator<S>>,
187:     ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
188:         let mut acc = AssignedAccumulator::assign(
189:             layouter,
190:             &self.curve_chip,
191:             &self.scalar_chip,
192:             1,
193:             1,
194:             &[],
195:             fixed_base_names,
196:             value,
197:         )?;
198: 
199:         let scalar_chip = &self.scalar_chip;
200: 
201:         scalar_chip.assert_equal_to_fixed(layouter, &acc.lhs.scalars[0].scalar, S::F::ONE)?;
202:         scalar_chip.assert_equal_to_fixed(layouter, &acc.rhs.scalars[0].scalar, S::F::ONE)?;
203:         acc.lhs.scalars[0].bound = BigUint::one();
204:         acc.rhs.scalars[0].bound = BigUint::one();
205: 
206:         Ok(acc)
207:     }
208: 
209:     /// Accumulates several accumulators together. The resulting acc will
210:     /// satisfy the invariant iff all the accumulators individually do.
211:     pub fn accumulate(
212:         &self,
213:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
214:         accs: &[AssignedAccumulator<S>],
215:     ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
216:         AssignedAccumulator::<S>::accumulate(
217:             layouter,
218:             self,
219:             &self.scalar_chip,
220:             &self.sponge_chip,
221:             accs,
222:         )
223:     }
224: }
225: 
226: impl<S: SelfEmulation> VerifierGadget<S> {
227:     /// Assigns a verifying key as a public input. All the necessary information
228:     /// is required off-circuit, except for the `transcript_repr` value.
229:     pub fn assign_vk_as_public_input(
230:         &self,
231:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
232:         vk_name: &str,
233:         domain: &EvaluationDomain<S::F>,
234:         cs: &ConstraintSystem<S::F>,
235:         transcript_repr_value: Value<S::F>,
236:     ) -> Result<AssignedVk<S>, Error> {
237:         let transcript_repr: AssignedNative<S::F> =
238:             self.scalar_chip.assign_as_public_input(layouter, transcript_repr_value)?;
239: 
240:         // We expect a finalized cs with no selectors, i.e. whose selectors have been
241:         // converted into fixed columns.
242:         let selectors = vec![vec![false]; cs.num_selectors()];
243:         let (processed_cs, _) = cs.clone().directly_convert_selectors_to_fixed(selectors);
244: 
245:         let assigned_vk = AssignedVk {
246:             vk_name: vk_name.to_string(),
247:             domain: domain.clone(),
248:             cs: processed_cs,
249:             transcript_repr,
250:         };
251: 
252:         Ok(assigned_vk)
253:     }
254: 
255:     /// Assigns a verifying key as a constant. All the necessary information is
256:     /// available off-circuit, except for the `transcript_repr` which is
257:     /// "assigned fixed".
258:     pub fn assign_fixed_vk(
259:         &self,
260:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
261:         vk_name: &str,
262:         domain: &EvaluationDomain<S::F>,
263:         cs: &ConstraintSystem<S::F>,
264:         transcript_repr_constant: S::F,
265:     ) -> Result<AssignedVk<S>, Error> {
266:         let transcript_repr = self.scalar_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, transcript_repr_constant)?;
267:         // We expect a finalized cs with no selectors, i.e. whose selectors have been
268:         // converted into fixed columns.
269:         let selectors = vec![vec![false]; cs.num_selectors()];
270:         let (processed_cs, _) = cs.clone().directly_convert_selectors_to_fixed(selectors);
271: 
272:         let assigned_vk = AssignedVk {
273:             vk_name: vk_name.to_string(),
274:             domain: domain.clone(),
275:             cs: processed_cs,
276:             transcript_repr,
277:         };
278: 
279:         Ok(assigned_vk)
280:     }
281: }
282: 
283: impl<S: SelfEmulation> VerifierGadget<S> {
284:     /// Given a plonk proof, this function parses it to extract the verifying
285:     /// trace.
286:     /// This function computes all Fiat-Shamir challenges, with the exception of
287:     /// `x`, which is computed in [Self::verify_algebraic_constraints]. It
288:     /// is the in-circuit analog of "parse_trace" from midnight-proofs at
289:     /// src/plonk/verifier.rs.
290:     ///
291:     /// The trace is considered to be valid if it satisfies the
292:     /// [algebraic
293:     /// constraints](crate::verifier::VerifierGadget::verify_algebraic_constraints),
294:     /// and the resulting accumulator satisfies the
295:     /// [invariant](crate::verifier::Accumulator::check).
296:     pub fn parse_trace(
297:         &self,
298:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
299:         assigned_vk: &AssignedVk<S>,
300:         assigned_committed_instances: &[S::AssignedPoint],
301:         assigned_instances: &[&[AssignedNative<S::F>]],
302:         proof: Value<Vec<u8>>,
303:     ) -> Result<(super::traces::VerifierTrace<S>, TranscriptGadget<S>), Error> {
304:         let cs = &assigned_vk.cs;
305: 
306:         // Check that instances matches the expected number of instance columns
307:         assert_eq!(
308:             cs.num_instance_columns(),
309:             assigned_committed_instances.len() + assigned_instances.len()
310:         );
311: 
312:         let mut transcript =
313:             TranscriptGadget::new(&self.scalar_chip, &self.curve_chip, &self.sponge_chip);
314: 
315:         transcript.init_with_proof(layouter, proof)?;
316: 
317:         // Hash verification key into transcript
318:         transcript.common_scalar(layouter, &assigned_vk.transcript_repr)?;
319: 
320:         assigned_committed_instances
321:             .iter()
322:             .try_for_each(|com| transcript.common_point(layouter, com))?;
323: 
324:         for instance in assigned_instances {
325:             let n = self.scalar_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, (instance.len() as u64).into())?;
326:             transcript.common_scalar(layouter, &n)?;
327:             instance.iter().try_for_each(|pi| transcript.common_scalar(layouter, pi))?;
328:         }
329: 
330:         // Assert that we only have one phase.
331:         // TODO: get rid of this assumption, we could support more than one phase.
332:         assert_eq!(cs.phases().count(), 1);
333: 
334:         // Hash the prover's advice commitments into the transcript and squeeze
335:         // challenges
336:         let advice_commitments = (0..cs.num_advice_columns())
337:             .map(|_| transcript.read_point(layouter))
338:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
339: 
340:         // Sample theta challenge for keeping lookup columns linearly independent
341:         let theta = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
342: 
343:         let lookups_permuted = cs
344:             .lookups()
345:             .iter()
346:             .map(|_| lookup::read_permuted_commitments(layouter, &mut transcript))
347:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
348: 
349:         let beta = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
350:         let gamma = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
351: 
352:         let permutation_committed =
353:             // Hash each permutation product commitment
354:             permutation::read_product_commitments(layouter, &mut transcript, cs)?;
355: 
356:         let lookups_committed = lookups_permuted
357:             .into_iter()
358:             .map(|lookup|
359:                 // Hash each lookup product commitment
360:                 lookup.read_product_commitment(layouter, &mut transcript))
361:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
362: 
363:         let trash_challenge = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
364: 
365:         let trashcans_committed = cs
366:             .trashcans()
367:             .iter()
368:             .map(|_| trash::read_committed(layouter, &mut transcript))
369:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
370: 
371:         let vanishing = vanishing::read_commitments_before_y(layouter, &mut transcript)?;
372: 
373:         // Sample y challenge, which keeps the gates linearly independent
374:         let y = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
375: 
376:         Ok((
377:             super::traces::VerifierTrace {
378:                 advice_commitments,
379:                 vanishing,
380:                 lookups: lookups_committed,
381:                 trashcans: trashcans_committed,
382:                 permutations: permutation_committed,
383:                 beta,
384:                 gamma,
385:                 theta,
386:                 trash_challenge,
387:                 y,
388:             },
389:             transcript,
390:         ))
391:     }
392: 
393:     /// Given a [super::traces::VerifierTrace], this function computes the
394:     /// opening challenge, x, and proceeds to verify the algebraic constraints
395:     /// with the claimed evaluations. This function does not verify the PCS
396:     /// proof.
397:     ///
398:     /// The proof is considered to be valid if the resulting accumulator
399:     /// satisfies the [invariant](crate::verifier::Accumulator::check)
400:     /// with respect to the relevant `tau_in_g2`.
401:     pub fn verify_algebraic_constraints(
402:         &self,
403:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
404:         assigned_vk: &AssignedVk<S>,
405:         trace: super::traces::VerifierTrace<S>,
406:         assigned_committed_instances: &[S::AssignedPoint],
407:         assigned_instances: &[&[AssignedNative<S::F>]],
408:         mut transcript: TranscriptGadget<S>,
409:     ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
410:         let cs = &assigned_vk.cs;
411:         let k = assigned_vk.domain.k();
412:         let nb_committed_instances = assigned_committed_instances.len();
413: 
414:         let super::traces::VerifierTrace {
415:             advice_commitments,
416:             vanishing,
417:             lookups,
418:             trashcans,
419:             permutations,
420:             beta,
421:             gamma,
422:             theta,
423:             trash_challenge,
424:             y,
425:         } = trace;
426: 
427:         let vanishing = vanishing.read_commitment_after_y(
428:             layouter,
429:             &mut transcript,
430:             assigned_vk.domain.get_quotient_poly_degree(),
431:         )?;
432: 
433:         // Sample x challenge, which is used to ensure the circuit is satisfied with
434:         // high probability
435:         let x = transcript.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
436: 
437:         let instance_evals = {
438:             let instance_queries = cs.instance_queries();
439:             let min_rotation = instance_queries.iter().map(|(_, rot)| rot.0).min().unwrap();
440:             let max_rotation = instance_queries.iter().map(|(_, rot)| rot.0).max().unwrap();
441: 
442:             let max_instance_len =
443:                 assigned_instances.iter().map(|instance| instance.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
444: 
445:             let l_i_s = evaluate_lagrange_polynomials(
446:                 layouter,
447:                 &self.scalar_chip,
448:                 1 << k,
449:                 assigned_vk.domain.get_omega(),
450:                 (-max_rotation)..(max_instance_len as i32 + min_rotation.abs()),
451:                 &x,
452:             )?;
453: 
454:             instance_queries
455:                 .iter()
456:                 .map(|(column, rotation)| {
457:                     if column.index() < nb_committed_instances {
458:                         transcript.read_scalar(layouter)
459:                     } else {
460:                         let instances = assigned_instances[column.index() - nb_committed_instances];
461:                         let offset = (max_rotation - rotation.0) as usize;
462:                         inner_product(
463:                             layouter,
464:                             &self.scalar_chip,
465:                             instances,
466:                             &l_i_s[offset..offset + instances.len()],
467:                         )
468:                     }
469:                 })
470:                 .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?
471:         };
472: 
473:         let advice_evals = (0..cs.advice_queries().len())
474:             .map(|_| transcript.read_scalar(layouter))
475:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
476: 
477:         let fixed_evals = (0..cs.fixed_queries().len())
478:             .map(|_| transcript.read_scalar(layouter))
479:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
480: 
481:         let vanishing = vanishing.evaluate_after_x(layouter, &mut transcript)?;
482: 
483:         let permutations_common =
484:             evaluate_permutation_common(layouter, &mut transcript, cs.permutation().columns.len())?;
485: 
486:         let permutations_evaluated = permutations.evaluate(layouter, &mut transcript)?;
487: 
488:         let lookups_evaluated = lookups
489:             .into_iter()
490:             .map(|lookup| lookup.evaluate(layouter, &mut transcript))
491:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
492: 
493:         let trashcans_evaluated = trashcans
494:             .into_iter()
495:             .map(|trash| trash.evaluate(layouter, &mut transcript))
496:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
497: 
498:         // This check ensures the circuit is satisfied so long as the polynomial
499:         // commitments open to the correct values.
500:         let vanishing = {
501:             let blinding_factors = cs.blinding_factors();
502: 
503:             let l_evals = evaluate_lagrange_polynomials(
504:                 layouter,
505:                 &self.scalar_chip,
506:                 1 << k,
507:                 assigned_vk.domain.get_omega(),
508:                 (-((blinding_factors + 1) as i32))..1,
509:                 &x,
510:             )?;
511:             assert_eq!(l_evals.len(), 2 + blinding_factors);
512:             let l_last = l_evals[0].clone();
513:             let l_blind = sum::<S::F>(layouter, &self.scalar_chip, &l_evals[1..=blinding_factors])?;
514:             let l_0 = l_evals[1 + blinding_factors].clone();
515: 
516:             // Compute the expected value of h(x)
517:             let expressions = {
518:                 let evaluated_gate_ids = {
519:                     let mut ids = vec![];
520:                     for gate in cs.gates().iter() {
521:                         for poly in gate.polynomials().iter() {
522:                             ids.push(eval_expression::<S>(
523:                                 layouter,
524:                                 &self.scalar_chip,
525:                                 &advice_evals,
526:                                 &fixed_evals,
527:                                 &instance_evals,
528:                                 poly,
529:                             )?)
530:                         }
531:                     }
532:                     ids
533:                 };
534:                 let evaluated_perm_ids = permutation_expressions(
535:                     layouter,
536:                     &self.scalar_chip,
537:                     cs,
538:                     &permutations_evaluated,
539:                     &permutations_common,
540:                     &advice_evals,
541:                     &fixed_evals,
542:                     &instance_evals,
543:                     &l_0,
544:                     &l_last,
545:                     &l_blind,
546:                     &beta,
547:                     &gamma,
548:                     &x,
549:                 )?;
550: 
551:                 let evaluated_lookup_ids = cs
552:                     .lookups()
553:                     .iter()
554:                     .enumerate()
555:                     .map(|(index, _)| {
556:                         lookup_expressions(
557:                             layouter,
558:                             &self.scalar_chip,
559:                             &lookups_evaluated[index].evaluated,
560:                             cs.lookups()[index].input_expressions(),
561:                             cs.lookups()[index].table_expressions(),
562:                             &advice_evals,
563:                             &fixed_evals,
564:                             &instance_evals,
565:                             &l_0,
566:                             &l_last,
567:                             &l_blind,
568:                             &theta,
569:                             &beta,
570:                             &gamma,
571:                         )
572:                     })
573:                     .collect::<Result<Vec<Vec<_>>, Error>>()?
574:                     .concat();
575: 
576:                 let evaluated_trashcan_ids = cs
577:                     .trashcans()
578:                     .iter()
579:                     .enumerate()
580:                     .map(|(index, _)| {
581:                         trash_expressions(
582:                             layouter,
583:                             &self.scalar_chip,
584:                             &trashcans_evaluated[index].evaluated,
585:                             cs.trashcans()[index].selector(),
586:                             cs.trashcans()[index].constraint_expressions(),
587:                             &advice_evals,
588:                             &fixed_evals,
589:                             &instance_evals,
590:                             &trash_challenge,
591:                         )
592:                     })
593:                     .collect::<Result<Vec<Vec<_>>, Error>>()?
594:                     .concat();
595: 
596:                 std::iter::empty()
597:                     // Evaluate the circuit using the custom gates provided
598:                     .chain(evaluated_gate_ids)
599:                     .chain(evaluated_perm_ids)
600:                     .chain(evaluated_lookup_ids)
601:                     .chain(evaluated_trashcan_ids)
602:                     .collect::<Vec<_>>()
603:             };
604:             let splitting_factor =
605:                 ArithInstructions::pow(&self.scalar_chip, layouter, &x, (1 << k) - 1)?;
606:             let xn = self.scalar_chip.mul(layouter, &x, &splitting_factor, None)?;
607:             vanishing.verify(
608:                 layouter,
609:                 &self.scalar_chip,
610:                 &expressions,
611:                 &y,
612:                 &xn,
613:                 &splitting_factor,
614:             )
615:         }?;
616: 
617:         let one = AssignedBoundedScalar::<S::F>::one(layouter, &self.scalar_chip)?;
618:         let omega = assigned_vk.domain.get_omega();
619:         let omega_inv = omega.invert().unwrap();
620:         let omega_last = omega_inv.pow([cs.blinding_factors() as u64 + 1]);
621:         let x_next = self.scalar_chip.mul_by_constant(layouter, &x, omega)?;
622:         let x_prev = self.scalar_chip.mul_by_constant(layouter, &x, omega_inv)?;
623:         let x_last = self.scalar_chip.mul_by_constant(layouter, &x, omega_last)?;
624: 
625:         // Gets the evaluation point for a query at the given rotation.
626:         let get_point = |rotation: &Rotation| -> &AssignedNative<S::F> {
627:             match rotation.0 {
628:                 -1 => &x_prev,
629:                 0 => &x,
630:                 1 => &x_next,
631:                 _ => panic!("We do not support other rotations"),
632:             }
633:         };
634: 
635:         let queries = iter::empty()
636:             .chain(
637:                 cs.advice_queries().iter().enumerate().map(|(query_index, &(column, rot))| {
638:                     VerifierQuery::<S>::new(
639:                         &one,
640:                         get_point(&rot),
641:                         CommitmentLabel::Advice(column.index()),
642:                         &advice_commitments[column.index()],
643:                         &advice_evals[query_index],
644:                     )
645:                 }),
646:             )
647:             .chain(cs.instance_queries().iter().enumerate().filter_map(
648:                 |(query_index, &(column, rot))| {
649:                     if column.index() < nb_committed_instances {
650:                         Some(VerifierQuery::<S>::new(
651:                             &one,
652:                             get_point(&rot),
653:                             CommitmentLabel::Instance(column.index()),
654:                             &assigned_committed_instances[column.index()],
655:                             &instance_evals[query_index],
656:                         ))
657:                     } else {
658:                         None
659:                     }
660:                 },
661:             ))
662:             .chain((permutations_evaluated).queries(&one, &x, &x_next, &x_last))
663:             .chain(
664:                 (lookups_evaluated.iter())
665:                     .flat_map(|lookup| lookup.queries(&one, &x, &x_next, &x_prev)),
666:             )
667:             .chain(trashcans_evaluated.iter().flat_map(|trash| trash.queries(&one, &x)))
668:             .chain(
669:                 cs.fixed_queries().iter().enumerate().map(|(query_index, &(col, rot))| {
670:                     VerifierQuery::new_fixed(
671:                         &one,
672:                         get_point(&rot),
673:                         CommitmentLabel::Fixed(col.index()),
674:                         &assigned_vk.fixed_commitment_name(col.index()),
675:                         &fixed_evals[query_index],
676:                     )
677:                 }),
678:             )
679:             .chain(
680:                 permutations_common.queries(
681:                     &(0..cs.permutation().columns.len())
682:                         .map(|i| assigned_vk.perm_commitment_name(i))
683:                         .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
684:                     &one,
685:                     &x,
686:                 ),
687:             )
688:             .chain(vanishing.queries(&one, &x));
689: 
690:         // We are now convinced the circuit is satisfied so long as the
691:         // polynomial commitments open to the correct values, which is true as long
692:         // as the following accumulator passes the invariant.
693:         let multiopen_check = kzg::multi_prepare::<_, S>(
694:             layouter,
695:             #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
696:             &self.curve_chip,
697:             &self.scalar_chip,
698:             &mut transcript,
699:             queries,
700:         )?;
701: 
702:         Ok(multiopen_check)
703:     }
704: 
705:     /// Prepares a plonk proof into a PCS instance that can be finalized or
706:     /// batched. It is responsibility of the verifier to check the validity of
707:     /// the instance columns. It is the in-circuit analog of "prepare" from
708:     /// midnight-proofs at src/plonk/verifier.rs.
709:     ///
710:     /// The proof is considered to be valid if the resulting accumulator
711:     /// satisfies the [invariant](crate::verifier::Accumulator::check)
712:     /// with respect to the relevant `tau_in_g2`.
713:     pub fn prepare(
714:         &self,
715:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
716:         assigned_vk: &AssignedVk<S>,
717:         assigned_committed_instances: &[S::AssignedPoint],
718:         assigned_instances: &[&[AssignedNative<S::F>]],
719:         proof: Value<Vec<u8>>,
720:     ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
721:         let (trace, transcript) = self.parse_trace(
722:             layouter,
723:             assigned_vk,
724:             assigned_committed_instances,
725:             assigned_instances,
726:             proof,
727:         )?;
728: 
729:         self.verify_algebraic_constraints(
730:             layouter,
731:             assigned_vk,
732:             trace,
733:             assigned_committed_instances,
734:             assigned_instances,
735:             transcript,
736:         )
737:     }
738: }
739:

750:     };
751:     use rand::SeedableRng;

754:     use super::*;
755:     use crate::{

758:             foreign::weierstrass_chip::{

765:                 pow2range::Pow2RangeChip,
766:             },
767:             foreign::FieldChip,
768:             native::NB_ARITH_COLS,
769:             NativeChip, NativeConfig, NativeGadget,
770:         },
771:         hash::poseidon::{
772:             PoseidonChip, PoseidonConfig, PoseidonState, NB_POSEIDON_ADVICE_COLS,
773:             NB_POSEIDON_FIXED_COLS,
774:         },
775:         instructions::{
776:             hash::{HashCPU, HashInstructions},
777:             AssignmentInstructions, EccInstructions,
778:         },
779:         testing_utils::FromScratch,
780:         types::{ComposableChip, Instantiable},
781:         verifier::{accumulator::Accumulator, BlstrsEmulation},
782:     };
783: 
784:     type S = BlstrsEmulation;
785: 
786:     type F = <S as SelfEmulation>::F;
787:     type C = <S as SelfEmulation>::C;
788: 
789:     type E = <S as SelfEmulation>::Engine;
790:     type CBase = <C as CircuitCurve>::Base;
791: 
792:     type NG = NativeGadget<F, P2RDecompositionChip<F>, NativeChip<F>>;
793: 
794:     const NB_INNER_INSTANCES: usize = 1;
795: 
796:     #[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
797:     pub struct InnerCircuit {
798:         poseidon_preimage: Value<[F; 2]>,
799:     }
800: 
801:     impl InnerCircuit {
802:         pub fn from_witness(witness: [F; 2]) -> Self {
803:             Self {
804:                 poseidon_preimage: Value::known(witness),
805:             }
806:         }
807:     }
808: 
809:     impl Circuit<F> for InnerCircuit {
810:         type Config = <PoseidonChip<F> as FromScratch<F>>::Config;
811: 
812:         type FloorPlanner = SimpleFloorPlanner;
813: 
814:         type Params = ();
815: 
816:         fn without_witnesses(&self) -> Self {
817:             unreachable!()
818:         }
819: 
820:         fn configure(meta: &mut ConstraintSystem<F>) -> Self::Config {
821:             let committed_instance_column = meta.instance_column();
822:             let instance_column = meta.instance_column();
823:             PoseidonChip::configure_from_scratch(
824:                 meta,
825:                 &mut vec![],
826:                 &mut vec![],
827:                 &[committed_instance_column, instance_column],
828:             )
829:         }
830: 
831:         fn synthesize(
832:             &self,
833:             config: Self::Config,
834:             mut layouter: impl Layouter<F>,
835:         ) -> Result<(), Error> {
836:             let native_chip = NativeChip::new_from_scratch(&config.0);
837:             let poseidon_chip = PoseidonChip::new_from_scratch(&config);
838: 
839:             let inputs = native_chip
840:                 .assign_many(&mut layouter, &self.poseidon_preimage.transpose_array())?;
841:             let output = poseidon_chip.hash(&mut layouter, &inputs)?;
842: 
843:             native_chip.constrain_as_public_input(&mut layouter, &output)?;
844: 
845:             native_chip.load_from_scratch(&mut layouter)?;
846:             poseidon_chip.load_from_scratch(&mut layouter)
847:         }
848:     }
849: 
850:     #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
851:     pub struct TestCircuit {
852:         inner_vk: (EvaluationDomain<F>, ConstraintSystem<F>, Value<F>), // (domain, cs, vk_repr)
853:         inner_committed_instance: Value<C>,
854:         inner_instances: Value<[F; NB_INNER_INSTANCES]>,
855:         inner_proof: Value<Vec<u8>>,
856:     }
857: 
858:     impl Circuit<F> for TestCircuit {
859:         type Config = (
860:             NativeConfig,
861:             P2RDecompositionConfig,
862:             ForeignWeierstrassEccConfig<C>,
863:             PoseidonConfig<F>,
864:         );
865:         type FloorPlanner = SimpleFloorPlanner;
866:         type Params = ();
867: 
868:         fn without_witnesses(&self) -> Self {
869:             unreachable!()
870:         }
871: 
872:         fn configure(meta: &mut ConstraintSystem<F>) -> Self::Config {
873:             let nb_advice_cols = nb_foreign_ecc_chip_columns::<F, C, C, NG>();
874:             let nb_fixed_cols = NB_ARITH_COLS + 4;
875: 
876:             let advice_columns: Vec<_> =
877:                 (0..nb_advice_cols).map(|_| meta.advice_column()).collect();
878:             let fixed_columns: Vec<_> = (0..nb_fixed_cols).map(|_| meta.fixed_column()).collect();
879:             let committed_instance_column = meta.instance_column();
880:             let instance_column = meta.instance_column();
881: 
882:             let native_config = NativeChip::configure(
883:                 meta,
884:                 &(
885:                     advice_columns[..NB_ARITH_COLS].try_into().unwrap(),
886:                     fixed_columns[..NB_ARITH_COLS + 4].try_into().unwrap(),
887:                     [committed_instance_column, instance_column],
888:                 ),
889:             );
890: 
891:             let nb_parallel_range_checks = NB_ARITH_COLS - 1;
892:             let max_bit_len = 16;
893:             let core_decomp_config = {
894:                 let pow2_config =
895:                     Pow2RangeChip::configure(meta, &advice_columns[1..=nb_parallel_range_checks]);
896:                 P2RDecompositionChip::configure(meta, &(native_config.clone(), pow2_config))
897:             };
898: 
899:             let base_config = FieldChip::<F, CBase, C, NG>::configure(
900:                 meta,
901:                 &advice_columns,
902:                 nb_parallel_range_checks,
903:                 max_bit_len,
904:             );
905:             let curve_config = ForeignWeierstrassEccChip::<F, C, C, NG, NG>::configure(
906:                 meta,
907:                 &base_config,
908:                 &advice_columns,
909:                 nb_parallel_range_checks,
910:                 max_bit_len,
911:             );
912: 
913:             let poseidon_config = PoseidonChip::configure(
914:                 meta,
915:                 &(
916:                     advice_columns[..NB_POSEIDON_ADVICE_COLS].try_into().unwrap(),
917:                     fixed_columns[..NB_POSEIDON_FIXED_COLS].try_into().unwrap(),
918:                 ),
919:             );
920: 
921:             (
922:                 native_config,
923:                 core_decomp_config,
924:                 curve_config,
925:                 poseidon_config,
926:             )
927:         }
928: 
929:         fn synthesize(
930:             &self,
931:             config: Self::Config,
932:             mut layouter: impl Layouter<F>,
933:         ) -> Result<(), Error> {
934:             let native_chip = <NativeChip<F> as ComposableChip<F>>::new(&config.0, &());
935:             let core_decomp_chip = P2RDecompositionChip::new(&config.1, &16);
936:             let native_gadget = NativeGadget::new(core_decomp_chip.clone(), native_chip.clone());
937:             let curve_chip =
938:                 ForeignWeierstrassEccChip::new(&config.2, &native_gadget, &native_gadget);
939:             let poseidon_chip = PoseidonChip::new(&config.3, &native_chip);
940: 
941:             let verifier_chip =
942:                 VerifierGadget::<S>::new(&curve_chip, &native_gadget, &poseidon_chip);
943: 
944:             let assigned_inner_vk: AssignedVk<S> = verifier_chip.assign_vk_as_public_input(
945:                 &mut layouter,
946:                 "inner_vk",
947:                 &self.inner_vk.0,
948:                 &self.inner_vk.1,
949:                 self.inner_vk.2,
950:             )?;
951: 
952:             let assigned_committed_instance = curve_chip
953:                 .assign_without_subgroup_check(&mut layouter, self.inner_committed_instance)?;
954: 
955:             let assigned_inner_pi = native_gadget
956:                 .assign_many(&mut layouter, &self.inner_instances.transpose_array())?;
957: 
958:             let mut inner_proof_acc = verifier_chip.prepare(
959:                 &mut layouter,
960:                 &assigned_inner_vk,
961:                 &[assigned_committed_instance],
962:                 &[&assigned_inner_pi],
963:                 self.inner_proof.clone(),
964:             )?;
965: 
966:             inner_proof_acc.collapse(&mut layouter, &curve_chip, &native_gadget)?;
967: 
968:             verifier_chip.constrain_as_public_input(&mut layouter, &inner_proof_acc)?;
969: 
970:             core_decomp_chip.load(&mut layouter)
971:         }
972:     }
973: 
974:     #[test]
975:     fn test_verify_proof() {
976:         let mut rng = ChaCha8Rng::from_seed([0u8; 32]);
977: 
978:         let inner_k = 10;
979:         let inner_params = ParamsKZG::unsafe_setup(inner_k, &mut rng);
980:         let inner_vk = keygen_vk_with_k(&inner_params, &InnerCircuit::default(), inner_k).unwrap();
981:         let inner_pk = keygen_pk(inner_vk.clone(), &InnerCircuit::default()).unwrap();
982: 
983:         let preimage = [F::random(&mut rng), F::random(&mut rng)];
984:         let output = <PoseidonChip<F> as HashCPU<F, F>>::hash(&preimage);
985:         let inner_public_inputs = vec![output];
986: 
987:         let inner_proof = {
988:             let mut transcript = CircuitTranscript::<PoseidonState<F>>::init();
989:             create_proof::<
990:                 F,
991:                 KZGCommitmentScheme<E>,
992:                 CircuitTranscript<PoseidonState<F>>,
993:                 InnerCircuit,
994:             >(
995:                 &inner_params,
996:                 &inner_pk,
997:                 &[InnerCircuit::from_witness(preimage)],
998:                 1,
999:                 &[&[&[], &inner_public_inputs]],
1000:                 &mut transcript,
1001:                 &mut rng,
1002:             )
1003:             .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("Problem creating the inner proof"));
1004:             transcript.finalize()
1005:         };
1006: 
1007:         let inner_dual_msm = {
1008:             let mut transcript =
1009:                 CircuitTranscript::<PoseidonState<F>>::init_from_bytes(&inner_proof);
1010:             prepare::<F, KZGCommitmentScheme<E>, CircuitTranscript<PoseidonState<F>>>(
1011:                 &inner_vk,
1012:                 &[&[C::identity()]],
1013:                 &[&[&inner_public_inputs]],
1014:                 &mut transcript,
1015:             )
1016:             .expect("Problem preparing the inner proof")
1017:         };
1018: 
1019:         let fixed_bases = crate::verifier::fixed_bases::<S>("inner_vk", &inner_vk);
1020: 
1021:         let mut inner_acc =
1022:             Accumulator::<S>::from_dual_msm(inner_dual_msm.clone(), "inner_vk", &fixed_bases);
1023: 
1024:         let inner_verifier_params = inner_params.verifier_params();
1025:         assert!(inner_dual_msm.check(&inner_verifier_params));
1026:         assert!(inner_acc.check(&inner_verifier_params, &fixed_bases));
1027: 
1028:         inner_acc.collapse();
1029: 
1030:         // The inner proof is ready.
1031:         // Now, let us make a proof that we know an inner proof.
1032: 
1033:         let mut public_inputs = AssignedVk::<S>::as_public_input(&inner_vk);
1034:         public_inputs.extend(AssignedAccumulator::as_public_input(&inner_acc));
1035: 
1036:         let circuit = TestCircuit {
1037:             inner_vk: (
1038:                 inner_vk.get_domain().clone(),
1039:                 inner_vk.cs().clone(),
1040:                 Value::known(inner_vk.transcript_repr()),
1041:             ),
1042:             inner_committed_instance: Value::known(C::identity()),
1043:             inner_instances: Value::known([output]),
1044:             inner_proof: Value::known(inner_proof),
1045:         };
1046: 
1047:         let prover =
1048:             MockProver::run(&circuit, vec![vec![], public_inputs]).expect("MockProver failed");
1049:         prover.assert_satisfied();
1050:     }
1051: }

31: /// This is an element of $\mathbb{G}_1$ represented in the affine coordinate
32: /// space. It is ideal to keep elements in this representation to reduce memory
33: /// usage and improve performance through the use of mixed curve model
34: /// arithmetic.
35: #[derive(Copy, Clone)]
36: #[repr(transparent)]
37: pub struct G1Affine(pub(crate) blst_p1_affine);
38: 
39: const COMPRESSED_SIZE: usize = 48;
40: const UNCOMPRESSED_SIZE: usize = 96;
41: 
42: pub const A: Fp = Fp::ZERO;
43: pub const B: Fp = Fp(blst_fp {
44:     // 0x04 in Montgomery form.
45:     l: [
46:         0xaa270000000cfff3,
47:         0x53cc0032fc34000a,
48:         0x478fe97a6b0a807f,
49:         0xb1d37ebee6ba24d7,
50:         0x8ec9733bbf78ab2f,
51:         0x09d645513d83de7e,
52:     ],
53: });

55: impl fmt::Debug for G1Affine {
56:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
57:         let is_ident: bool = self.is_identity().into();
58:         f.debug_struct("G1Affine")
59:             .field("x", &self.x())
60:             .field("y", &self.y())
61:             .field("infinity", &is_ident)
62:             .finish()
63:     }
64: }
65: 
66: impl fmt::Display for G1Affine {
67:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
68:         if self.is_identity().into() {
69:             write!(f, "G1Affine(Infinity)")
70:         } else {
71:             write!(f, "G1Affine(x={}, y={})", self.x(), self.y())
72:         }
73:     }
74: }
75: 
76: impl core::hash::Hash for G1Affine {
77:     fn hash<H: core::hash::Hasher>(&self, state: &mut H) {
78:         self.0.x.l.hash::<H>(state);
79:         self.0.y.l.hash::<H>(state)
80:     }
81: }

354: 
355:     /// Attempts to deserialize an uncompressed element, not checking if the
356:     /// element is on the curve and not checking if it is in the correct
357:     /// subgroup.
358:     ///
359:     /// **This is dangerous to call unless you trust the bytes you are reading;
360:     /// otherwise, API invariants may be broken.** Please consider using
361:     /// `from_uncompressed()` instead.
362:     fn from_uncompressed_unchecked(bytes: &[u8; UNCOMPRESSED_SIZE]) -> CtOption<Self> {
363:         let mut raw = blst_p1_affine::default();
364:         let success =
365:             unsafe { blst_p1_deserialize(&mut raw, bytes.as_ptr()) == BLST_ERROR::BLST_SUCCESS };
366:         CtOption::new(G1Affine(raw), Choice::from(success as u8))
367:     }
368: 
369:     /// Attempts to deserialize a compressed element.
370:     fn from_compressed(bytes: &[u8; COMPRESSED_SIZE]) -> CtOption<Self> {
371:         G1Affine::from_compressed_unchecked(bytes)
372:             .and_then(|p| CtOption::new(p, p.is_torsion_free()))
373:     }
374: 
375:     /// Attempts to deserialize an uncompressed element, not checking if the
376:     /// element is in the correct subgroup.
377:     ///
378:     /// **This is dangerous to call unless you trust the bytes you are reading;
379:     /// otherwise, API invariants may be broken.** Please consider using
380:     /// `from_compressed()` instead.
381:     fn from_compressed_unchecked(bytes: &[u8; COMPRESSED_SIZE]) -> CtOption<Self> {
382:         let mut raw = blst_p1_affine::default();
383:         let success =
384:             unsafe { blst_p1_uncompress(&mut raw, bytes.as_ptr()) == BLST_ERROR::BLST_SUCCESS };
385:         CtOption::new(G1Affine(raw), Choice::from(success as u8))
386:     }

26: /// A multi-scalar multiplication in the polynomial commitment scheme.
27: /// For every i, term (bases_i, scalars_i) may be have an optional
28: /// label_i for debugging or other purposes.
29: #[derive(Clone, Default, Debug)]
30: pub struct MSMKZG<E: Engine> {
31:     pub(crate) scalars: Vec<E::Fr>,
32:     pub(crate) bases: Vec<E::G1>,
33:     pub(crate) labels: Vec<CommitmentLabel>,
34: }
35: 
36: impl<E: Engine> MSMKZG<E> {
37:     /// Create an empty MSM instance
38:     pub fn init() -> Self {
39:         MSMKZG {
40:             scalars: vec![],
41:             bases: vec![],
42:             labels: vec![],
43:         }
44:     }
45: 
46:     /// Create an MSM from various MSMs
47:     pub fn from_many(msms: Vec<Self>) -> Self {
48:         let len = msms.iter().map(|m| m.scalars.len()).sum();
49: 
50:         let mut scalars = Vec::with_capacity(len);
51:         let mut bases = Vec::with_capacity(len);
52:         let mut labels = Vec::with_capacity(len);
53: 
54:         for mut msm in msms {
55:             scalars.append(&mut msm.scalars);
56:             bases.append(&mut msm.bases);
57:             labels.append(&mut msm.labels);
58:         }
59: 
60:         Self {
61:             scalars,
62:             bases,
63:             labels,
64:         }
65:     }
66: 
67:     /// Create a new MSM from a given base (with scalar of 1).
68:     pub fn from_base(base: &E::G1) -> Self {
69:         MSMKZG {
70:             scalars: vec![E::Fr::ONE],
71:             bases: vec![*base],
72:             labels: vec![CommitmentLabel::NoLabel],
73:         }
74:     }
75: }
76: 
77: impl<E: Engine + Debug> MSMKZG<E>
78: where
79:     E::G1Affine: CurveAffine<ScalarExt = E::Fr, CurveExt = E::G1>,
80: {
81:     /// Evaluates the MSM to a single point and replaces all terms with that
82:     /// single point (scalar = 1, label = `NoLabel`).
83:     ///
84:     /// This mirrors `AssignedMsm::collapse` in the circuits crate.
85:     ///
86:     /// # Panics (in debug mode)
87:     ///
88:     /// If any term carries a label other than `NoLabel` or `Advice`.
89:     //
90:     // We only allow `NoLabel` or `Advice` because these types of labels are
91:     // not relevant for the `verifier_gadget` in `midnight-circuits` (at least for
92:     // now). Other types of labels may carry information that we do not want to lose
93:     // when "collapsing".
94:     pub fn collapse(&mut self) {
95:         debug_assert!(
96:             self.labels
97:                 .iter()
98:                 .all(|l| matches!(l, CommitmentLabel::NoLabel | CommitmentLabel::Advice(_))),
99:             "collapse: all labels must be NoLabel or Advice, found: {:?}",
100:             self.labels,
101:         );
102:         let point = self.eval();
103:         self.scalars = vec![E::Fr::ONE];
104:         self.bases = vec![point];
105:         self.labels = vec![CommitmentLabel::NoLabel];
106:     }
107: }
108: 
109: impl<E: Engine + Debug> MSM<E::G1Affine> for MSMKZG<E>
110: where
111:     E::G1Affine: CurveAffine<ScalarExt = E::Fr, CurveExt = E::G1>,
112: {
113:     fn append_term(&mut self, scalar: E::Fr, point: E::G1, label: CommitmentLabel) {
114:         self.scalars.push(scalar);
115:         self.bases.push(point);
116:         self.labels.push(label);
117:     }
118: 
119:     fn add_msm(&mut self, other: &Self) {
120:         self.scalars.reserve(other.scalars().len());
121:         self.scalars.extend_from_slice(&other.scalars());
122: 
123:         self.bases.reserve(other.bases().len());
124:         self.bases.extend_from_slice(&other.bases());
125: 
126:         self.labels.reserve(other.labels().len());
127:         self.labels.extend_from_slice(&other.labels());
128:     }
129: 
130:     fn scale(&mut self, factor: E::Fr) {
131:         self.scalars.par_iter_mut().for_each(|s| {
132:             *s *= &factor;
133:         })
134:     }
135: 
136:     fn check(&self) -> bool {
137:         bool::from(self.eval().is_identity())
138:     }
139: 
140:     fn eval(&self) -> E::G1 {
141:         if self.scalars == vec![E::Fr::ONE] {
142:             self.bases[0]
143:         } else {
144:             msm_specific::<E::G1Affine>(&self.scalars, &self.bases)
145:         }
146:     }
147: 
148:     fn bases(&self) -> Vec<E::G1> {
149:         self.bases.clone()
150:     }
151: 
152:     fn scalars(&self) -> Vec<E::Fr> {
153:         self.scalars.clone()
154:     }
155: 
156:     fn labels(&self) -> Vec<CommitmentLabel> {
157:         self.labels.clone()
158:     }
159: }

161: #[allow(unsafe_code)]
162: /// Wrapper over the MSM function to use the blstrs underlying function
163: pub fn msm_specific<C: CurveAffine>(coeffs: &[C::Scalar], bases: &[C::Curve]) -> C::Curve {
164:     // We remove zeros (keep only non-zero coefficients)
165:     let (coeffs, bases): (Vec<C::Scalar>, Vec<C::Curve>) = coeffs
166:         .iter()
167:         .zip(bases)
168:         .filter(|(s, _)| !s.is_zero_vartime())
169:         .map(|(s, b)| (*s, *b))
170:         .unzip();
171: 
172:     if coeffs.is_empty() {
173:         return C::Curve::identity();
174:     }
175: 
176:     // We empirically checked that for MSMs larger than 2**18, the blstrs
177:     // implementation regresses.
178:     if coeffs.len() <= (2 << 18) && TypeId::of::<C>() == TypeId::of::<midnight_curves::G1Affine>() {
179:         // Safe: we just checked type
180:         let coeffs_slice = coeffs.as_slice();
181:         let bases_slice = bases.as_slice();
182:         let coeffs = unsafe { &*(coeffs_slice as *const _ as *const [Fq]) };
183:         let bases = unsafe { &*(bases_slice as *const _ as *const [G1Projective]) };
184:         let res = G1Projective::multi_exp(bases, coeffs);
185:         unsafe { std::mem::transmute_copy(&res) }
186:     } else {
187:         let mut affine_bases = vec![C::identity(); coeffs.len()];
188:         C::Curve::batch_normalize(&bases, &mut affine_bases);
189:         msm_best(&coeffs, &affine_bases)
190:     }
191: }

236: impl<E: MultiMillerLoop + Debug> DualMSM<E>
237: where
238:     E::G1Affine: CurveAffine<ScalarExt = E::Fr, CurveExt = E::G1>,
239: {
240:     /// Create an empty two channel MSM accumulator instance
241:     pub fn init() -> Self {
242:         Self {
243:             left: MSMKZG::init(),
244:             right: MSMKZG::init(),
245:         }
246:     }
247: 
248:     /// Create a new two channel MSM accumulator instance
249:     pub fn new(left: MSMKZG<E>, right: MSMKZG<E>) -> Self {
250:         Self { left, right }
251:     }
252: 
253:     /// Split the [DualMSM] into `left` and `right`
254:     pub fn split(&self) -> SplitDualMSM<'_, E> {
255:         let left = izip!(
256:             self.left.labels.iter(),
257:             self.left.scalars.iter(),
258:             self.left.bases.iter()
259:         )
260:         .collect();
261:         let right = izip!(
262:             self.right.labels.iter(),
263:             self.right.scalars.iter(),
264:             self.right.bases.iter(),
265:         )
266:         .collect();
267:         (left, right)
268:     }
269: 
270:     /// Scale all scalars in the MSM by some scaling factor
271:     pub fn scale(&mut self, e: E::Fr) {
272:         self.left.scale(e);
273:         self.right.scale(e);
274:     }
275: 
276:     /// Add another multiexp into this one
277:     pub fn add_msm(&mut self, other: Self) {
278:         self.left.add_msm(&other.left);
279:         self.right.add_msm(&other.right);
280:     }
281: 
282:     /// Performs final pairing check with given verifier params and two channel
283:     /// linear combination
284:     pub fn check(self, params: &ParamsVerifierKZG<E>) -> bool {
285:         let left = if self.left.scalars.len() == 1 && self.left.scalars[0] == E::Fr::ONE {
286:             self.left.bases[0]
287:         } else {
288:             self.left.eval()
289:         };
290: 
291:         let right = self.right.eval();
292: 
293:         let (term_1, term_2) = (
294:             (&left.into(), &params.s_g2_prepared),
295:             (&right.into(), &params.n_g2_prepared),
296:         );
297:         let terms = &[term_1, term_2];
298: 
299:         bool::from(E::multi_miller_loop(&terms[..]).final_exponentiation().is_identity())
300:     }
301: }

14:     type Input = Vec<u8>;
15:     type Output = Vec<u8>;
16: 
17:     fn init() -> Self {
18:         Params::new().hash_length(64).key(b"Domain separator for transcript").to_state()
19:     }

22:         self.update(&[BLAKE2B_PREFIX_COMMON]);
23:         self.update(input);

32: /// Custom Blake2b-256 transcript hash that accumulates data in a `Vec<u8>`
33: /// and resets to the hash output on squeeze, keeping the state compact.
34: ///
35: /// This is designed for on-chain verification (e.g. Cardano/Plutus), where
36: /// blake2b-256 is available as a native built-in and memory efficiency matters.
37: ///
38: /// Only BLS12-381 types (`G1Projective`, `Fq`) implement `Hashable` and
39: /// `Sampleable` for this hash, as it targets the Midnight proof system.
40: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
41: pub struct Blake2b256 {
42:     transcript_data: Vec<u8>,
43: }
44: 
45: impl TranscriptHash for Blake2b256 {
46:     type Input = Vec<u8>;
47:     type Output = Vec<u8>;
48: 
49:     fn init() -> Self {
50:         Self {
51:             transcript_data: vec![],
52:         }
53:     }
54: 
55:     fn absorb(&mut self, input: &Self::Input) {
56:         self.transcript_data.extend_from_slice(input);
57:     }
58: 
59:     fn squeeze(&mut self) -> Self::Output {
60:         let h = Params::new().hash_length(32).hash(&self.transcript_data);
61:         let result = h.as_bytes().to_vec();
62:         self.transcript_data = result.clone();
63:         result
64:     }
65: }
66: 
67: impl<T: TranscriptHash<Input = Vec<u8>>> Hashable<T> for u32 {
68:     fn to_input(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
69:         self.to_le_bytes().to_vec()
70:     }
71: 
72:     fn to_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
73:         self.to_le_bytes().to_vec()
74:     }
75: 
76:     fn read(buffer: &mut impl Read) -> io::Result<Self> {
77:         let mut bytes = [0u8; 4];
78:         buffer.read_exact(&mut bytes)?;
79:         Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(bytes))
80:     }
81: }
82:

85:     /// Converts it to compressed form in bytes
86:     fn to_input(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
87:         Hashable::to_bytes(self)
88:     }
89: 
90:     fn to_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8> {
91:         <Self as GroupEncoding>::to_bytes(self).as_ref().to_vec()
92:     }

95:         let mut bytes = <Self as GroupEncoding>::Repr::default();
96:

98: 
99:         Option::from(Self::from_bytes(&bytes))
100:             .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("Invalid BN point encoding in proof"))
101:     }

112:     }
