59: 
60: /// In-circuit analog of
61: /// [`KZGCommitment`](midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::commitment::KZGCommitment).
62: ///
63: /// Carries a polynomial commitment (or a lazy linear combination of them)
64: /// together with its `PolynomialLabel`(s).
65: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
66: pub enum AssignedKZGCommitment<S: SelfEmulation> {
67:     /// A single committed point with its label.
68:     Simple(AssignedPoint<S>, PolynomialLabel),
69:     /// A lazy linear combination `∑ scalars[i] * points[i]` with per-term
70:     /// labels, accumulated during verification for MSM batching.
71:     Linear(
72:         Vec<AssignedPoint<S>>,
73:         Vec<AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>>,
74:         Vec<PolynomialLabel>,
75:     ),
76: }
77: 
78: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InnerValue for AssignedKZGCommitment<S> {
79:     type Element = KZGCommitment<S::Engine>;
80: 
81:     fn value(&self) -> Value<Self::Element> {
82:         match self.clone() {
83:             Self::Simple(p, label) => {
84:                 p.value().map(|p| KZGCommitment::Simple(*p.get_point(), label))
85:             }
86:             Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => {
87:                 let points: Vec<Value<S::C>> =
88:                     points.iter().map(|p| p.value().map(|p| *p.get_point())).collect();
89:                 let scalars: Vec<Value<S::F>> =
90:                     scalars.iter().map(|s| s.scalar.value().copied()).collect();
91:                 Value::from_iter(points)
92:                     .zip(Value::from_iter(scalars))
93:                     .map(|(ps, ss)| KZGCommitment::Linear(ps, ss, labels))
94:             }
95:         }
96:     }
97: }
98: 
99: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedKZGCommitment<S> {
100:     /// Creates a `Simple` commitment from a variable-base assigned point and
101:     /// label.
102:     pub fn simple(point: S::AssignedPoint, label: PolynomialLabel) -> Self {
103:         AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(AssignedPoint::Variable(point), label)
104:     }
105: 
106:     /// Creates a `Simple` commitment for a globally-known constant point.
107:     ///
108:     /// No circuit cell is allocated for the point; it is identified by its
109:     /// label and will be looked up from a fixed-bases map when the
110:     /// accumulator is resolved.
111:     pub fn fixed(label: PolynomialLabel) -> Self {
112:         AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(AssignedPoint::Fixed, label)
113:     }
114: 
115:     /// Assigns a curve point in the circuit and wraps it in a labeled `Simple`
116:     /// commitment.
117:     pub fn assign(
118:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
119:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
120:         point: Value<S::C>,
121:         label: PolynomialLabel,
122:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
123:         curve_chip.assign(layouter, point).map(|p| Self::simple(p, label))
124:     }
125: 
126:     /// Converts this commitment into an [`AssignedMsm`] for use in the
127:     /// multiopen accumulation.
128:     ///
129:     /// `Simple(p, l)` becomes a one-term MSM `[(1, p, l)]`.
130:     /// `Linear(points, scalars, labels)` becomes the corresponding multi-term
131:     /// MSM.
132:     pub fn into_msm(
133:         self,
134:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
135:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
136:     ) -> Result<AssignedMsm<S>, Error> {
137:         let one = AssignedBoundedScalar::one(layouter, scalar_chip)?;
138:         Ok(match self {
139:             Self::Simple(p, label) => AssignedMsm::new(&[one], &[p], &[label]),
140:             Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => AssignedMsm::new(&scalars, &points, &labels),
141:         })
142:     }
143: }
144: 
145: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedKZGCommitment<S> {
146:     /// Scales this commitment by a scalar.
147:     ///
148:     /// `Simple(p, l)` becomes `Linear([p], [scalar], [l])`.
149:     /// For `Linear`, all existing scalars are multiplied by `scalar`.
150:     fn mul(
151:         self,
152:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
153:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
154:         scalar: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
155:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
156:         let scalar = AssignedBoundedScalar::new(scalar, None);
157:         match self {
158:             Self::Simple(p, label) => Ok(Self::Linear(vec![p], vec![scalar], vec![label])),
159:             Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => Ok(Self::Linear(
160:                 points,
161:                 scalars
162:                     .iter()
163:                     .map(|s| mul_bounded_scalars(layouter, scalar_chip, s, &scalar))
164:                     .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?,
165:                 labels,
166:             )),
167:         }
168:     }
169: 
170:     /// Adds two commitments, merging them into a `Linear` combination.
171:     fn add(
172:         self,
173:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
174:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
175:         other: Self,
176:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
177:         let one = AssignedBoundedScalar::one(layouter, scalar_chip)?;
178:         let (mut points, mut scalars, mut labels) = match self {
179:             Self::Simple(p, label) => (vec![p], vec![one.clone()], vec![label]),
180:             Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => (points, scalars, labels),
181:         };
182:         let (other_points, other_scalars, other_labels) = match other {
183:             Self::Simple(p, label) => (vec![p], vec![one.clone()], vec![label]),
184:             Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => (points, scalars, labels),
185:         };
186:         points.extend(other_points);
187:         scalars.extend(other_scalars);
188:         labels.extend(other_labels);
189:         Ok(Self::Linear(points, scalars, labels))
190:     }
191: }
192: 
193: /// In-circuit analog of
194: /// [`KZGMultiCommitment`](midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::commitment::KZGMultiCommitment):
195: /// a commitment to one or more polynomials.
196: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
197: pub struct AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S: SelfEmulation>(pub Vec<AssignedKZGCommitment<S>>);
198: 
199: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S> {
200:     fn assert_single(&self) {
201:         assert_eq!(
202:             self.0.len(),
203:             1,
204:             "operation on AssignedKZGMultiCommitment requires exactly one polynomial"
205:         );
206:     }
207: 
208:     /// Returns the single inner [`AssignedKZGCommitment`], panicking if this
209:     /// commitment does not hold exactly one polynomial.
210:     pub(crate) fn into_single(self) -> AssignedKZGCommitment<S> {
211:         self.assert_single();
212:         self.0.into_iter().next().unwrap()
213:     }
214: 
215:     /// In-circuit commitment to the zero polynomial (the identity point),
216:     /// tagged with `label`. Used e.g. for empty committed-instance columns.
217:     pub fn commitment_to_zero(
218:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
219:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
220:         label: PolynomialLabel,
221:     ) -> Result<Self, Error>
222:     where
223:         S::CurveChip: AssignmentInstructions<S::F, S::AssignedPoint>,
224:     {
225:         let point = curve_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, S::C::identity())?;
226:         Ok(Self(vec![AssignedKZGCommitment::simple(point, label)]))
227:     }
228: }
229: 
230: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InnerValue for AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S> {
231:     type Element = midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::commitment::KZGMultiCommitment<S::Engine>;
232: 
233:     fn value(&self) -> Value<Self::Element> {
234:         Value::from_iter(self.0.iter().map(|c| c.value()))
235:             .map(midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::commitment::KZGMultiCommitment)
236:     }
237: }
238: 
239: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InCircuitHomomorphicCommitment<S> for AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S> {
240:     fn mul(
241:         self,
242:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
243:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
244:         scalar: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
245:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
246:         self.assert_single();
247:         let inner = self.0.into_iter().next().unwrap().mul(layouter, scalar_chip, scalar)?;
248:         Ok(Self(vec![inner]))
249:     }
250: 
251:     fn add(
252:         self,
253:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
254:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
255:         other: Self,
256:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
257:         self.assert_single();
258:         other.assert_single();
259:         let inner = self.0.into_iter().next().unwrap().add(
260:             layouter,
261:             scalar_chip,
262:             other.0.into_iter().next().unwrap(),
263:         )?;
264:         Ok(Self(vec![inner]))
265:     }
266: }
267: 
268: // --------------------------------
269: // See proofs/src/poly/kzg/utils.rs
270: // --------------------------------
271: 
272: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
273: pub(crate) struct CommitmentData<S: SelfEmulation> {
274:     label: PolynomialLabel,
275:     set_index: usize,
276:     point_indices: Vec<usize>,
277:     evals: Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>,
278: }
279: 
280: impl<S: SelfEmulation> CommitmentData<S> {
281:     fn new(label: PolynomialLabel) -> Self {
282:         CommitmentData {
283:             label,
284:             set_index: 0,
285:             point_indices: vec![],
286:             evals: vec![],
287:         }
288:     }
289: }
290: 
291: type IntermediateSets<S> = (
292:     Vec<CommitmentData<S>>,
293:     Vec<Vec<AssignedNative<<S as SelfEmulation>::F>>>,
294: );
295: 
296: #[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]
297: fn construct_intermediate_sets<S: SelfEmulation>(
298:     queries: &[(PolynomialLabel, AssignedNative<S::F>, AssignedNative<S::F>)],
299:     default_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
300: ) -> Result<IntermediateSets<S>, Error> {
301:     // Construct sets of unique commitments and corresponding information about
302:     // their queries.
303:     let mut commitment_map: Vec<CommitmentData<S>> = vec![];
304: 
305:     // Also construct mapping from a unique point to a point_index. This defines
306:     // an ordering on the points.
307:     // Note that we use a HashMap, whereas halo2 uses a BTreeMap. This is because
308:     // `AssignedScalar` does not implement `Ord`, but implements `Hash`.
309:     // This difference is not a problem, since the order of keys does not matter
310:     // for this algorithm.
311:     let mut point_index_map = HashMap::new();
312: 
313:     // Iterate over all of the queries, computing the ordering of the points
314:     // while also creating new commitment data.
315:     for (query_label, query_point, _query_eval) in queries.iter() {
316:         let num_points = point_index_map.len();
317:         let point_idx = point_index_map.entry(query_point).or_insert(num_points);
318: 
319:         if let Some(pos) = commitment_map.iter().position(|comm| &comm.label == query_label) {
320:             if commitment_map[pos].point_indices.contains(point_idx) {
321:                 return Err(Error::Synthesis("repeated query".into()));
322:             }
323:             commitment_map[pos].point_indices.push(*point_idx);
324:         } else {
325:             let mut tmp = CommitmentData::new(query_label.clone());
326:             tmp.point_indices.push(*point_idx);
327:             commitment_map.push(tmp);
328:         }
329:     }
330: 
331:     // Also construct inverse mapping from point_index to the point
332:     let mut inverse_point_index_map = HashMap::new();
333:     for (&point, &point_index) in point_index_map.iter() {
334:         inverse_point_index_map.insert(point_index, point.clone());
335:     }
336: 
337:     // Construct map of unique ordered point_idx_sets to their set_idx.
338:     let mut point_idx_sets = HashMap::new();
339:     // Also construct mapping from commitment to point_idx_set
340:     let mut commitment_set_map = Vec::new();
341: 
342:     for commitment_data in commitment_map.iter() {
343:         let mut point_index_set = BTreeSet::new();
344:         // Note that point_index_set is ordered, unlike point_indices
345:         for &point_index in commitment_data.point_indices.iter() {
346:             point_index_set.insert(point_index);
347:         }
348: 
349:         // Push point_index_set to CommitmentData for the relevant commitment
350:         commitment_set_map.push((commitment_data.label.clone(), point_index_set.clone()));
351: 
352:         let num_sets = point_idx_sets.len();
353:         point_idx_sets.entry(point_index_set).or_insert(num_sets);
354:     }
355: 
356:     // Initialise empty evals vec for each unique commitment
357:     for commitment_data in commitment_map.iter_mut() {
358:         let len = commitment_data.point_indices.len();
359:         commitment_data.evals = vec![default_eval.clone(); len];
360:     }
361: 
362:     // Populate set_index, evals and points for each commitment using point_idx_sets
363:     for (query_label, query_point, query_eval) in queries.iter() {
364:         // The index of the point at which the commitment is queried
365:         let point_index = point_index_map.get(&query_point).unwrap();
366: 
367:         // The point_index_set at which the commitment was queried
368:         let mut point_index_set = BTreeSet::new();
369:         for (l, point_idx_set) in commitment_set_map.iter() {
370:             if l == query_label {
371:                 point_index_set.clone_from(point_idx_set);
372:             }
373:         }
374:         assert!(!point_index_set.is_empty());
375: 
376:         // The set_index of the point_index_set
377:         let set_index = point_idx_sets.get(&point_index_set).unwrap();
378:         for commitment_data in commitment_map.iter_mut() {
379:             if query_label == &commitment_data.label {
380:                 commitment_data.set_index = *set_index;
381:             }
382:         }
383:         let point_index_set: Vec<usize> = point_index_set.iter().cloned().collect();
384: 
385:         // The offset of the point_index in the point_index_set
386:         let point_index_in_set = point_index_set.iter().position(|i| i == point_index).unwrap();
387: 
388:         for commitment_data in commitment_map.iter_mut() {
389:             if *query_label == commitment_data.label {
390:                 // Insert the eval using the ordering of the point_index_set
391:                 commitment_data.evals[point_index_in_set] = query_eval.clone();
392:             }
393:         }
394:     }
395: 
396:     // Get actual points in each point set
397:     let mut point_sets: Vec<Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>> = vec![Vec::new(); point_idx_sets.len()];
398:     for (point_idx_set, &set_idx) in point_idx_sets.iter() {
399:         for &point_idx in point_idx_set.iter() {
400:             let point = inverse_point_index_map.get(&point_idx).unwrap();
401:             point_sets[set_idx].push((*point).clone());
402:         }
403:     }
404: 
405:     Ok((commitment_map, point_sets))
406: }
407: 
408: // ----------------------------------
409: // See proofs/src/utils/arithmetic.rs
410: // ----------------------------------
411: 
412: fn msm_inner_product<S: SelfEmulation>(
413:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
414:     scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
415:     msms: &[AssignedMsm<S>],
416:     scalars: &[AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>],
417: ) -> Result<AssignedMsm<S>, Error> {
418:     let mut res = AssignedMsm::empty();
419:     let mut msms = msms.to_vec();
420:     for (msm, s) in msms.iter_mut().zip(scalars) {
421:         msm.scale(layouter, scalar_chip, s)?;
422:         res.add_msm(msm)?;
423:     }
424:     Ok(res)
425: }
426: 
427: /// Computes the inner product of a set of polynomial evaluations and a set of
428: /// scalar values. This function computes the weighted sum of polynomial
429: /// evaluations. Each vector in `evals_set` is multiplied element-wise by a
430: /// corresponding scalar from `scalars`, and the results are accumulated
431: /// into a single vector.
432: fn evals_inner_product<F: CircuitField>(
433:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<F>,
434:     scalar_chip: &impl ArithInstructions<F, AssignedNative<F>>,
435:     evals_set: &[Vec<AssignedNative<F>>],
436:     scalars: &[AssignedBoundedScalar<F>],
437: ) -> Result<Vec<AssignedNative<F>>, Error> {
438:     let zero = scalar_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, F::ZERO)?;
439:     let mut res = vec![zero.clone(); evals_set[0].len()];
440:     for (poly_evals, s) in evals_set.iter().zip(scalars) {
441:         for i in 0..res.len() {
442:             // res[i] := s.scalar * poly_evals[i] + res[i]
443:             res[i] = mul_add(layouter, scalar_chip, &s.scalar, &poly_evals[i], &res[i])?;
444:         }
445:     }
446:     Ok(res)
447: }
448: 
449: // ------------------------------
450: // See proofs/src/poly/kzg/mod.rs
451: // ------------------------------
452: 
453: /// Verifies a bunch of KZG queries in a multi-open argument.
454: /// The resulting accumulator satisfies the invariant iff all queries are valid.
455: pub(crate) fn multi_prepare_kzg<S: SelfEmulation>(
456:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
457:     #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")] curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
458:     scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
459:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
460:     queries: &[VerifierQuery<S, InCircuitKZG<S>>],
461: ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
462:     let one = AssignedBoundedScalar::one(layouter, scalar_chip)?;
463: 
464:     // Add dummy queries to reduce the number of distinct multi-open point sets.
465:     #[cfg(feature = "fewer-point-sets")]
466:     let queries = &{
467:         let pairs: Vec<_> = queries.iter().map(|q| (q.label.clone(), q.point.clone())).collect();
468:         let dummy_openings = midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::compute_dummy_queries(&pairs);
469:         let mut queries = queries.to_vec();
470:         for (idx, dummy_point) in dummy_openings {
471:             queries.push(VerifierQuery {
472:                 point: dummy_point,
473:                 commitment: queries[idx].commitment,
474:                 label: queries[idx].label.clone(),
475:                 eval: transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?,
476:             });
477:         }
478:         queries
479:     };
480: 
481:     let x1 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
482:     let x2 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
483: 
484:     // Peel each query's multi-commitment down to the single inner commitment it
485:     // targets, keyed by the query label. A length-1 commitment (the common case,
486:     // including the `Linear` linearization commitment) peels to its sole inner;
487:     // a batched commitment holds several `Simple`s, so we pick the one whose own
488:     // label matches the query.
489:     let label_to_commitment: HashMap<PolynomialLabel, &AssignedKZGCommitment<S>> = queries
490:         .iter()
491:         .map(|q| {
492:             let inners = &q.commitment.0;
493:             let inner = if inners.len() == 1 {
494:                 &inners[0]
495:             } else {
496:                 inners
497:                     .iter()
498:                     .find(|c| matches!(c, AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(_, label) if *label == q.label))
499:                     .expect("batched commitment has no polynomial matching the query label")
500:             };
501:             (q.label.clone(), inner)
502:         })
503:         .collect();
504: 
505:     let default_eval = scalar_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, S::F::default())?;
506:     let kzg_queries = queries
507:         .iter()
508:         .map(|query| (query.label.clone(), query.point.clone(), query.eval.clone()))
509:         .collect::<Vec<_>>();
510:     let (commitment_map, point_sets) =
511:         construct_intermediate_sets::<S>(&kzg_queries, default_eval)?;
512: 
513:     let mut q_coms: Vec<_> = vec![vec![]; point_sets.len()];
514:     let mut q_eval_sets = vec![vec![]; point_sets.len()];
515: 
516:     for com_data in commitment_map.into_iter() {
517:         let mut msm = AssignedMsm::empty();
518:         match label_to_commitment[&com_data.label] {
519:             AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(p, label) => msm.add_term(&one, p, label),
520:             AssignedKZGCommitment::Linear(points, scalars, labels) => {
521:                 msm.add_msm(&AssignedMsm::new(scalars, points, labels))?;
522:             }
523:         }
524:         q_coms[com_data.set_index].push(msm);
525:         q_eval_sets[com_data.set_index].push(com_data.evals);
526:     }
527: 
528:     let truncated_x1_powers = {
529:         let nb_x1_powers = q_coms.iter().map(|v| v.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
530:         assert!(nb_x1_powers >= q_eval_sets.iter().map(|v| v.len()).max().unwrap_or(0));
531:         truncated_powers(layouter, scalar_chip, &x1, nb_x1_powers)?
532:     };
533: 
534:     let q_coms = q_coms
535:         .iter()
536:         .map(|msms| msm_inner_product(layouter, scalar_chip, msms, &truncated_x1_powers))
537:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
538: 
539:     let q_eval_sets = q_eval_sets
540:         .iter()
541:         .map(|evals| evals_inner_product(layouter, scalar_chip, evals, &truncated_x1_powers))
542:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
543: 
544:     // Sort point sets by ascending cardinality to ensure the first set is the one
545:     // that contains fixed commitments (which are evaluated at x only). This
546:     // property is not necessary for the actual proving system, but it is important
547:     // for in-circuit verification of proofs. (It enables an optimization based on
548:     // an internal collapse.)
549:     //
550:     // The (len, i) key provides a deterministic total order even when two sets
551:     // share the same cardinality.
552:     let (q_coms, q_eval_sets, point_sets) = {
553:         let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..point_sets.len()).collect();
554:         order.sort_by_key(|&i| (point_sets[i].len(), i));
555:         let q_coms: Vec<_> = order.iter().map(|&i| q_coms[i].clone()).collect();
556:         let q_eval_sets: Vec<_> = order.iter().map(|&i| q_eval_sets[i].clone()).collect();
557:         let point_sets: Vec<_> = order.iter().map(|&i| point_sets[i].clone()).collect();
558:         (q_coms, q_eval_sets, point_sets)
559:     };
560: 
561:     let f_com = transcript_gadget
562:         .read_commitment(layouter, &[PolynomialLabel::Custom("kzg_batch".into())])?
563:         .into_single();
564: 
565:     let x3 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
566:     #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
567:     let x3 = truncate::<S::F>(layouter, scalar_chip, &x3)?;
568:     #[cfg(not(feature = "truncated-challenges"))]
569:     let x3 = AssignedBoundedScalar::new(&x3, None);
570: 
571:     let mut q_evals_on_x3 = Vec::with_capacity(q_eval_sets.len());
572:     for _ in 0..q_eval_sets.len() {
573:         q_evals_on_x3.push(transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?);
574:     }
575: 
576:     let zero = scalar_chip.assign_fixed(layouter, S::F::ZERO)?;
577:     let f_eval = point_sets
578:         .iter()
579:         .zip(q_eval_sets.iter())
580:         .zip(q_evals_on_x3.iter())
581:         .rev()
582:         .try_fold(zero, |acc_eval, ((points, evals), proof_eval)| {
583:             let r_eval =
584:                 evaluate_interpolated_polynomial(layouter, scalar_chip, points, evals, &x3.scalar)?;
585: 
586:             // eval = (proof_eval - r_eval) / prod_i (x3 - point_i)
587:             let den = points.iter().skip(1).try_fold(
588:                 scalar_chip.sub(layouter, &x3.scalar, &points[0])?,
589:                 |acc, point| {
590:                     // acc * (x3 - point) computed as acc * x3 - acc * point
591:                     scalar_chip.add_and_double_mul(
592:                         layouter,
593:                         (S::F::ZERO, &acc),
594:                         (S::F::ZERO, &x3.scalar),
595:                         (S::F::ZERO, point),
596:                         S::F::ZERO,
597:                         (S::F::ONE, -S::F::ONE),
598:                     )
599:                 },
600:             )?;
601:             let mut eval = scalar_chip.sub(layouter, proof_eval, &r_eval)?;
602:             eval = scalar_chip.div(layouter, &eval, &den)?;
603: 
604:             // acc_eval * x2 + eval
605:             mul_add(layouter, scalar_chip, &acc_eval, &x2, &eval)
606:         })?;
607: 
608:     let x4 = transcript_gadget.squeeze_challenge(layouter)?;
609:     let truncated_x4_powers =
610:         truncated_powers::<S::F>(layouter, scalar_chip, &x4, q_coms.len() + 1)?;
611: 
612:     let final_com = {
613:         let mut coms = q_coms;
614: 
615:         // We collapse all AssignedMsm at this point to later leverage the fact that x4
616:         // powers are truncated. Exceptionally, the first one is not collapsed,
617:         // as the first x4 power is 1.
618:         #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
619:         coms.iter_mut().skip(1).try_for_each(|com| {
620:             com.collapse(layouter, curve_chip, scalar_chip, PolynomialLabel::NoLabel)
621:         })?;
622:         coms.push(f_com.into_msm(layouter, scalar_chip)?);
623: 
624:         msm_inner_product(layouter, scalar_chip, &coms, &truncated_x4_powers)?
625:     };
626: 
627:     let v = {
628:         let mut evals = q_evals_on_x3;
629:         evals.push(f_eval);
630: 
631:         let scalar_x4_powers: Vec<_> =
632:             truncated_x4_powers.iter().map(|s| s.scalar.clone()).collect();
633: 
634:         AssignedBoundedScalar::new(
635:             &inner_product(layouter, scalar_chip, &evals, &scalar_x4_powers)?,
636:             None,
637:         )
638:     };
639: 
640:     let pi = transcript_gadget
641:         .read_commitment(layouter, &[PolynomialLabel::Custom("π".into())])?
642:         .into_single();
643:     let pi_msm = pi.into_msm(layouter, scalar_chip)?;
644: 
645:     // Scale zπ
646:     let mut scaled_pi = pi_msm.clone();
647:     scaled_pi.scale(layouter, scalar_chip, &x3)?;
648: 
649:     // (π, C − vG + zπ)
650:     let left = pi_msm; // π
651: 
652:     let right = {
653:         let mut right = final_com; // C
654:         let minus_v_gen = AssignedMsm::from_fixed_term(v, PolynomialLabel::Custom("-G".into()));
655:         right.add_msm(&minus_v_gen)?; // -vG
656:         right.add_msm(&scaled_pi)?; // zπ
657:         right
658:     };
659: 
660:     Ok(AssignedAccumulator::new(left, right))
661: }
662: 
663: /// KZG instantiation of [`InCircuitPCS`].
664: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
665: pub struct InCircuitKZG<S: SelfEmulation>(PhantomData<S>);
666: 
667: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InCircuitPCS<S> for InCircuitKZG<S> {
668:     type AssignedCommitment = AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S>;
669: 
670:     fn fixed_commitment(label: PolynomialLabel) -> Self::AssignedCommitment {
671:         AssignedKZGMultiCommitment(vec![AssignedKZGCommitment::fixed(label)])
672:     }
673: 
674:     fn read_commitment(
675:         transcript: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
676:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
677:         labels: &[PolynomialLabel],
678:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedCommitment, Error> {
679:         transcript.read_commitment(layouter, labels)
680:     }
681: 
682:     fn assign_commitment(
683:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
684:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
685:         value: Value<S::C>,
686:         label: PolynomialLabel,
687:     ) -> Result<Self::AssignedCommitment, Error> {
688:         Ok(AssignedKZGMultiCommitment(vec![
689:             AssignedKZGCommitment::assign(layouter, curve_chip, value, label)?,
690:         ]))
691:     }
692: 
693:     fn common_commitment(
694:         transcript: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
695:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
696:         commitment: &AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S>,
697:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
698:         transcript.common_commitment(layouter, commitment)
699:     }
700: 
701:     fn multi_prepare(
702:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
703:         _curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
704:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
705:         transcript: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
706:         queries: &[VerifierQuery<'_, S, Self>],
707:     ) -> Result<AssignedAccumulator<S>, Error> {
708:         multi_prepare_kzg(
709:             layouter,
710:             #[cfg(feature = "truncated-challenges")]
711:             _curve_chip,
712:             scalar_chip,
713:             transcript,
714:             queries,
715:         )
716:     }
717: }

14: //! In-circuit lookup argument verification.
15: //!
16: //! This is the in-circuit analog of `proofs/src/plonk/logup/verifier.rs`.
17: //! The constraint expressions are implemented in `expressions/lookup.rs`.
18: 
19: use midnight_proofs::{circuit::Layouter, plonk::Error, poly::PolynomialLabel};
20: 
21: use crate::{
22:     field::AssignedNative,
23:     verifier::{
24:         SelfEmulation,
25:         pcs::{InCircuitPCS, VerifierQuery},
26:         transcript_gadget::TranscriptGadget,
27:     },
28: };
29: 
30: /// Commitment to the multiplicity columns, read from the transcript.
31: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
32: pub(crate) struct CommittedMultiplicities<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
33:     multiplicities: PCS::AssignedCommitment,
34: }
35: 
36: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
37: pub(crate) struct LookupEvaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
38:     pub(crate) multiplicities_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
39:     pub(crate) helper_evals: Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>,
40:     pub(crate) accumulator_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
41:     pub(crate) accumulator_next_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
42: }
43: 
44: /// Commitments to the LogUp polynomials, read from the transcript.
45: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
46: pub(crate) struct Committed<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
47:     argument_index: usize,
48:     multiplicities: PCS::AssignedCommitment,
49:     helper_polys: Vec<PCS::AssignedCommitment>,
50:     accumulator: PCS::AssignedCommitment,
51: }
52: 
53: /// Commitments plus evaluations at challenge point.
54: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
55: pub(crate) struct Evaluated<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
56:     committed: Committed<S, PCS>,
57:     pub(crate) evaluated: LookupEvaluated<S>,
58: }
59: 
60: /// Reads the prover's multiplicities commitment from the transcript.
61: pub(crate) fn read_multiplicities<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>>(
62:     argument_index: usize,
63:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
64:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
65: ) -> Result<CommittedMultiplicities<S, PCS>, Error> {
66:     let multiplicities = PCS::read_commitment(
67:         transcript_gadget,
68:         layouter,
69:         &[PolynomialLabel::LogupMultiplicities(argument_index)],
70:     )?;
71:     Ok(CommittedMultiplicities { multiplicities })
72: }
73: 
74: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> CommittedMultiplicities<S, PCS> {
75:     pub(crate) fn read_commitment(
76:         self,
77:         argument_index: usize,
78:         nb_flattened: usize,
79:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
80:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
81:     ) -> Result<Committed<S, PCS>, Error> {
82:         let helper_polys = (0..nb_flattened)
83:             .map(|j| {
84:                 PCS::read_commitment(
85:                     transcript_gadget,
86:                     layouter,
87:                     &[PolynomialLabel::LogupHelper(argument_index, j)],
88:                 )
89:             })
90:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
91:         let accumulator = PCS::read_commitment(
92:             transcript_gadget,
93:             layouter,
94:             &[PolynomialLabel::LogupAggregator(argument_index)],
95:         )?;
96: 
97:         Ok(Committed {
98:             argument_index,
99:             multiplicities: self.multiplicities,
100:             helper_polys,
101:             accumulator,
102:         })
103:     }
104: }
105: 
106: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> Committed<S, PCS> {
107:     pub(crate) fn evaluate(
108:         self,
109:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
110:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
111:     ) -> Result<Evaluated<S, PCS>, Error> {
112:         let nb_flattened = self.helper_polys.len();
113:         let multiplicities_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
114:         let helper_evals = (0..nb_flattened)
115:             .map(|_| transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter))
116:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
117:         let accumulator_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
118:         let accumulator_next_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
119: 
120:         Ok(Evaluated {
121:             committed: self,
122:             evaluated: LookupEvaluated {
123:                 multiplicities_eval,
124:                 helper_evals,
125:                 accumulator_eval,
126:                 accumulator_next_eval,
127:             },
128:         })
129:     }
130: }
131: 
132: // "expressions" is implemented in `expressions/lookup.rs`
133: 
134: impl<'a, S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> Evaluated<S, PCS> {
135:     pub(crate) fn queries(
136:         &'a self,
137:         x: &AssignedNative<S::F>,      // evaluation point x
138:         x_next: &AssignedNative<S::F>, // ωx
139:     ) -> Vec<VerifierQuery<'a, S, PCS>> {
140:         let arg = self.committed.argument_index;
141:         let mut queries = vec![
142:             // Open lookup product commitment at x
143:             VerifierQuery::new(
144:                 x,
145:                 &self.committed.multiplicities,
146:                 PolynomialLabel::LogupMultiplicities(arg),
147:                 &self.evaluated.multiplicities_eval,
148:             ),
149:         ];
150:         // Open lookup input commitments at x
151:         for (j, (h_commit, h_eval)) in self
152:             .committed
153:             .helper_polys
154:             .iter()
155:             .zip(self.evaluated.helper_evals.iter())
156:             .enumerate()
157:         {
158:             queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
159:                 x,
160:                 h_commit,
161:                 PolynomialLabel::LogupHelper(arg, j),
162:                 h_eval,
163:             ));
164:         }
165:         // Open lookup table commitments at x
166:         queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
167:             x,
168:             &self.committed.accumulator,
169:             PolynomialLabel::LogupAggregator(arg),
170:             &self.evaluated.accumulator_eval,
171:         ));
172:         // Open lookup product commitment at \omega x
173:         queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
174:             x_next,
175:             &self.committed.accumulator,
176:             PolynomialLabel::LogupAggregator(arg),
177:             &self.evaluated.accumulator_next_eval,
178:         ));
179:         queries
180:     }
181: }

57: /// Type for in-circuit verifying keys.
58: ///
59: /// This type carries off-circuit a lot of the information about the vk.
60: /// The only in-circuit field is the `transcript_repr`.
61: ///
62: /// The only entry-point for this function is intended to be
63: /// [VerifierGadget::assign_vk_as_public_input]. This is possible because fixed
64: /// commitments are dealt with off-circuit, i.e., the resulting accumulator of
65: /// [VerifierGadget::prepare] contains the scalars of the
66: /// fixed-commitments, in the `fixed_base_scalars` field (of its RHS).
67: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
68: pub struct AssignedVk<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
69:     domain: EvaluationDomain<S::F>,
70:     fixed_commitments: Vec<PCS::AssignedCommitment>,
71:     perm_commitments: Vec<PCS::AssignedCommitment>,
72:     cs: ConstraintSystem<S::F>,
73:     cs_degree: usize,
74:     transcript_repr: AssignedNative<S::F>,
75: }
76: 
77: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> InnerValue for AssignedVk<S, PCS> {
78:     type Element = VerifyingKey<S>;
79: 
80:     fn value(&self) -> Value<VerifyingKey<S>> {
81:         unimplemented!(
82:             "It is not possible to get a full verifying key out of an
83:              AssignedVk, as the latter does not include fixed commitments."
84:         )
85:     }
86: }
87: 
88: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> Instantiable<S::F> for AssignedVk<S, PCS> {
89:     fn as_public_input(vk: &VerifyingKey<S>) -> Vec<S::F> {
90:         AssignedNative::<S::F>::as_public_input(&vk.transcript_repr())
91:     }
92: 
93:     #[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
94:     fn from_public_input(_fields: &[S::F]) -> Option<VerifyingKey<S>> {
95:         unimplemented!("as_public_input encodes the VK as its transcript_repr() — not invertible")
96:     }
97: }

99: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> AssignedVk<S, PCS> {
100:     /// The assigned `transcript_repr` cell of this verifying key.
101:     pub fn transcript_repr(&self) -> &AssignedNative<S::F> {
102:         &self.transcript_repr
103:     }
104: }
105: 
106: /// Builds the map from [`PolynomialLabel`] to curve point for all
107: /// circuit-constant bases of a verifying key.
108: ///
109: /// The map contains:
110: /// * `Fixed(i)`: the i-th fixed-column commitment,
111: /// * `PermutationFixed(i)`: the i-th permutation commitment,
112: /// * `Custom("-G")`: the negated designated generator used in the KZG opening
113: ///   proof.
114: ///
115: /// Pass this map to [`Accumulator::check`] or [`Msm::eval`].
116: pub fn fixed_bases<S: SelfEmulation>(vk: &VerifyingKey<S>) -> BTreeMap<PolynomialLabel, S::C> {
117:     let mut fixed_bases = BTreeMap::new();
118: 
119:     let fixed_commitments = vk.fixed_commitments();
120:     let perm_commitments = vk.permutation().commitments();
121: 
122:     for (i, com) in fixed_commitments.iter().enumerate() {
123:         fixed_bases.insert(PolynomialLabel::Fixed(i), *com.0[0].as_point());
124:     }
125: 
126:     for (i, com) in perm_commitments.iter().enumerate() {
127:         fixed_bases.insert(PolynomialLabel::PermutationFixed(i), *com.0[0].as_point());
128:     }
129: 
130:     fixed_bases.insert(PolynomialLabel::Custom("-G".into()), -S::C::generator());
131: 
132:     fixed_bases
133: }
134: 
135: /// Returns the ordered list of [`PolynomialLabel`]s for the fixed bases of a
136: /// circuit with the given number of fixed and permutation commitments.
137: ///
138: /// The order matches [`fixed_bases`]: fixed columns first, then permutation
139: /// columns, then `Custom("-G")`. Call this before having an actual verifying
140: /// key (e.g. during setup) to size an accumulator correctly.
141: pub fn fixed_base_labels<S: SelfEmulation>(
142:     nb_fixed_commitments: usize,
143:     nb_perm_commitments: usize,
144: ) -> Vec<PolynomialLabel> {
145:     let mut labels = Vec::with_capacity(nb_fixed_commitments + nb_perm_commitments + 1);
146: 
147:     for i in 0..nb_fixed_commitments {
148:         labels.push(PolynomialLabel::Fixed(i));
149:     }
150: 
151:     for i in 0..nb_perm_commitments {
152:         labels.push(PolynomialLabel::PermutationFixed(i));
153:     }
154: 
155:     // This term will be introduced by the KZG multiopen argument as a fixed base.
156:     // It corresponds to the negated designated generator. It is not proper of the
157:     // verifying key, but there is no harm in having it here (it needs to be
158:     // introduced at some point anyway and this is a good place).
159:     labels.push(PolynomialLabel::Custom("-G".into()));
160: 
161:     labels
162: }

14: //! A module for in-circuit partial MSMs and its off-circuit analog.
15: //! These MSM have a fixed-base part which is represented by the corresponding
16: //! scalars only.

41: /// An off-circuit base in a multi-scalar multiplication.
42: ///
43: /// `Variable` stores the concrete curve point inline.
44: /// `Fixed` indicates that the point is a globally-known constant (e.g. a
45: /// verifying-key commitment, an SRS element, or the negated generator `-G`)
46: /// identified by its `PolynomialLabel`; the actual value is not stored here
47: /// and must be supplied when the MSM is evaluated or resolved.
48: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
49: pub enum Point<S: SelfEmulation> {
50:     /// A concrete curve point whose value is known at this stage.
51:     Variable(S::C),
52:     /// A globally-known constant point, identified by its label.
53:     Fixed,
54: }
55: 
56: /// The in-circuit analog of [`Point`].
57: ///
58: /// `Variable` holds an explicitly-assigned circuit cell.
59: /// `Fixed` means the base is a known constant (e.g. a VK-committed point, an
60: /// SRS element, or the negated generator); no cell is allocated for it in the
61: /// layout.
62: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
63: pub enum AssignedPoint<S: SelfEmulation> {
64:     /// A circuit-assigned variable-base point.
65:     Variable(S::AssignedPoint),
66:     /// A globally-known constant point that lives outside the circuit witness.
67:     Fixed,
68: }
69: 
70: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Point<S> {
71:     /// Returns a reference to the inner curve point.
72:     ///
73:     /// # Panics
74:     ///
75:     /// If this is a `Fixed` point whose value has not yet been resolved.
76:     /// Call `resolve_fixed_bases` before accessing the point.
77:     pub fn get_point(&self) -> &S::C {
78:         match self {
79:             Self::Variable(p) => p,
80:             Self::Fixed => {
81:                 panic!("attempted to read an unresolved Fixed base; call resolve_fixed_bases first")
82:             }
83:         }
84:     }
85: }
86: 
87: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InnerValue for AssignedPoint<S> {
88:     type Element = Point<S>;
89: 
90:     fn value(&self) -> Value<Self::Element> {
91:         match self {
92:             AssignedPoint::Variable(p) => p.value().map(|v| Point::Variable(v)),
93:             AssignedPoint::Fixed => Value::known(Point::Fixed),
94:         }
95:     }
96: }
97: 
98: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Instantiable<S::F> for AssignedPoint<S> {
99:     fn as_public_input(point: &Point<S>) -> Vec<S::F> {
100:         match point {
101:             Point::Variable(p) => S::AssignedPoint::as_public_input(p),
102:             Point::Fixed => vec![],
103:         }
104:     }
105: 
106:     #[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
107:     fn from_public_input(fields: &[S::F]) -> Option<Point<S>> {
108:         match fields {
109:             [] => Some(Point::Fixed),
110:             fs => S::AssignedPoint::from_public_input(fs).map(Point::Variable),
111:         }
112:     }
113: }
114: 
115: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedPoint<S> {
116:     pub(crate) fn in_circuit_as_public_input(
117:         &self,
118:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
119:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
120:     ) -> Result<Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>, Error> {
121:         match self {
122:             AssignedPoint::Variable(p) => curve_chip.as_public_input(layouter, p),
123:             AssignedPoint::Fixed => Ok(vec![]),
124:         }
125:     }
126: 
127:     pub(crate) fn constrain_as_public_input(
128:         &self,
129:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
130:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
131:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
132:         match self {
133:             AssignedPoint::Variable(p) => curve_chip.constrain_as_public_input(layouter, p),
134:             AssignedPoint::Fixed => Ok(()),
135:         }
136:     }
137: }

139: /// Off-circuit multi-scalar multiplication with mixed fixed and variable bases.
140: ///
141: /// This is the off-circuit analog of [`AssignedMsm`] and a generalization of
142: /// `MSMKZG` that can refer to some bases as "fixed" (globally-known constants).
143: /// The triple `(scalars, bases, labels)` is flat and parallel:
144: /// `scalars[i]` multiplies `bases[i]`, which is identified by `labels[i]`.
145: ///
146: /// A `Fixed` base is not stored inline; its actual curve point is supplied
147: /// later via `resolve_fixed_bases` or `eval`.
148: ///
149: /// Invariant: `scalars.len() == bases.len() == labels.len()`.
150: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
151: pub struct Msm<S: SelfEmulation> {
152:     scalars: Vec<S::F>,
153:     bases: Vec<Point<S>>,
154:     labels: Vec<PolynomialLabel>,
155: }
156: 
157: /// Type for in-circuit multi-scalar multiplications.
158: ///
159: /// This is the in-circuit analog of `Msm<C>`.
160: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
161: pub struct AssignedMsm<S: SelfEmulation> {
162:     scalars: Vec<AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>>,
163:     bases: Vec<AssignedPoint<S>>,
164:     labels: Vec<PolynomialLabel>,
165: }
166: 
167: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Msm<S> {
168:     /// Creates a new MSM from the given slice of bases, scalars and labels.
169:     ///
170:     /// # Panics
171:     ///
172:     /// If `bases`, `scalars` and `labels` do not all have the same length.
173:     pub fn new(bases: &[Point<S>], scalars: &[S::F], labels: &[PolynomialLabel]) -> Self {
174:         assert_eq!(bases.len(), scalars.len());
175:         assert_eq!(bases.len(), labels.len());
176:         Msm {
177:             bases: bases.to_vec(),
178:             scalars: scalars.to_vec(),
179:             labels: labels.to_vec(),
180:         }
181:     }
182: 
183:     /// Builds an `Msm` from a slice of `(label, scalar, base)` triples, as
184:     /// returned by [`midnight_proofs::poly::kzg::msm::DualMSM::split`].
185:     ///
186:     /// Each base is classified as `Fixed` if its label appears in
187:     /// `fixed_bases` (with a sanity-check that the point matches the expected
188:     /// value), or `Variable` otherwise.
189:     pub fn from_terms(
190:         terms: &[(&PolynomialLabel, &S::F, &S::C)],
191:         fixed_bases: &BTreeMap<PolynomialLabel, S::C>,
192:     ) -> Self {
193:         let mut bases = Vec::with_capacity(terms.len());
194:         let mut scalars = Vec::with_capacity(terms.len());
195:         let mut labels = Vec::with_capacity(terms.len());
196: 
197:         for &(label, scalar, base) in terms {
198:             scalars.push(*scalar);
199:             labels.push(label.clone());
200:             match fixed_bases.get(label) {
201:                 Some(expected) => {
202:                     assert_eq!(*base, *expected);
203:                     bases.push(Point::Fixed);
204:                 }
205:                 None => bases.push(Point::Variable(*base)),
206:             }
207:         }
208:         Msm::new(&bases, &scalars, &labels)
209:     }
210: 
211:     /// The bases of this MSM.
212:     pub fn bases(&self) -> Vec<Point<S>> {
213:         self.bases.clone()
214:     }
215: 
216:     /// The scalars of this MSM.
217:     pub fn scalars(&self) -> Vec<S::F> {
218:         self.scalars.clone()
219:     }
220: 
221:     /// The labels of this MSM.
222:     pub fn labels(&self) -> Vec<PolynomialLabel> {
223:         self.labels.clone()
224:     }
225: 
226:     /// Collapses the variable-base part into a single `(collapsed-point, 1)`
227:     /// term, labeling the result with `label`.
228:     ///
229:     /// All `Variable` entries are accumulated into one curve point via MSM and
230:     /// stored with scalar `1` and the given `label`.
231:     /// `Fixed` entries are preserved: terms sharing the same label have their
232:     /// scalars summed. After the call the MSM has exactly one `Variable` entry
233:     /// (the collapsed result) plus one `Fixed` entry per distinct fixed label.
234:     ///
235:     /// This function mutates `self`.
236:     pub fn collapse(&mut self, label: PolynomialLabel) {
237:         // We allocate max capacity but we may not fill it.
238:         let n = self.bases.len();
239:         let mut variable_bases = Vec::<S::G1Affine>::with_capacity(n);
240:         let mut variable_scalars = Vec::<S::F>::with_capacity(n);
241: 
242:         let mut fixed_base_scalars = BTreeMap::<PolynomialLabel, S::F>::new();
243: 
244:         for i in 0..n {
245:             match self.bases[i] {
246:                 Point::Variable(p) => {
247:                     variable_bases.push(p.into());
248:                     variable_scalars.push(self.scalars[i]);
249:                 }
250:                 Point::Fixed => {
251:                     fixed_base_scalars
252:                         .entry(self.labels[i].clone())
253:                         .and_modify(|s| *s += self.scalars[i])
254:                         .or_insert(self.scalars[i]);
255:                 }
256:             }
257:         }
258: 
259:         let mut bases = vec![Point::Fixed; fixed_base_scalars.len()];
260:         let mut scalars: Vec<_> = fixed_base_scalars.values().copied().collect();
261:         let mut labels: Vec<_> = fixed_base_scalars.keys().cloned().collect();
262: 
263:         let collapsed_base = msm_best(&variable_scalars, &variable_bases);
264:         bases.push(Point::Variable(collapsed_base));
265:         scalars.push(S::F::ONE);
266:         labels.push(label);
267: 
268:         self.bases = bases;
269:         self.scalars = scalars;
270:         self.labels = labels;
271:     }
272: 
273:     /// Resolves all `Fixed` bases by looking up their curve points in the map.
274:     ///
275:     /// Each `Fixed` entry is replaced with `Variable(fixed_bases[label])`.
276:     /// After this call, no `Fixed` entries remain.
277:     ///
278:     /// # Panics
279:     ///
280:     /// If any `Fixed` label is absent from `fixed_bases`.
281:     pub fn resolve_fixed_bases(&mut self, fixed_bases: &BTreeMap<PolynomialLabel, S::C>) {
282:         for (i, base) in self.bases.iter_mut().enumerate() {
283:             match base {
284:                 Point::Variable(..) => (),
285:                 Point::Fixed => {
286:                     let l = &self.labels[i];
287:                     let p = fixed_bases.get(l).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("Base not provided: {l}"));
288:                     *base = Point::Variable(*p)
289:                 }
290:             }
291:         }
292:     }
293: 
294:     /// Fully evaluates the MSM to a single curve point.
295:     ///
296:     /// Equivalent to calling `resolve_fixed_bases(fixed_bases)` followed by
297:     /// `collapse()`. Returns `∑ scalars[i] * bases[i]`, with all fixed bases
298:     /// substituted from the map.
299:     ///
300:     /// # Panics
301:     ///
302:     /// If any `Fixed` label is absent from `fixed_bases`.
303:     pub fn eval(&self, fixed_bases: &BTreeMap<PolynomialLabel, S::C>) -> S::C {
304:         let mut msm = self.clone();
305:         msm.resolve_fixed_bases(fixed_bases);
306:         msm.collapse(PolynomialLabel::NoLabel);
307: 
308:         debug_assert_eq!(msm.scalars, vec![S::F::ONE]);
309:         debug_assert_eq!(msm.labels, vec![PolynomialLabel::NoLabel]);
310: 
311:         match msm.bases.as_slice() {
312:             [Point::Variable(p)] => *p,
313:             _ => unreachable!(),
314:         }
315:     }
316: 
317:     /// Accumulates two MSMs with the given scalar r.
318:     /// The resulting MSM evaluates (on any `fixed_bases`) to
319:     /// `self.eval(fixed_bases) + r * other.eval(fixed_bases)`.
320:     pub fn accumulate_with_r(&self, other: &Self, r: S::F) -> Self {
321:         let mut acc = self.clone();
322: 
323:         acc.bases.extend(other.bases.clone());
324:         acc.scalars.extend(other.scalars.iter().map(|s| *s * r));
325:         acc.labels.extend(other.labels.clone());
326: 
327:         acc
328:     }
329: }
330: 
331: impl<S: SelfEmulation> InnerValue for AssignedMsm<S> {
332:     type Element = Msm<S>;
333: 
334:     fn value(&self) -> Value<Self::Element> {
335:         let bases: Value<Vec<Point<S>>> =
336:             Value::from_iter(self.bases.iter().map(|base| base.value()));
337: 
338:         let scalars: Value<Vec<S::F>> =
339:             Value::from_iter(self.scalars.iter().map(|s| s.scalar.value().copied()));
340: 
341:         let labels = self.labels.clone();
342: 
343:         scalars.zip(bases).map(|(scalars, bases)| Msm {
344:             bases,
345:             scalars,
346:             labels,
347:         })
348:     }
349: }
350: 
351: impl<S: SelfEmulation> Instantiable<S::F> for AssignedMsm<S> {
352:     fn as_public_input(msm: &Msm<S>) -> Vec<S::F> {
353:         [
354:             msm.bases
355:                 .iter()
356:                 .flat_map(AssignedPoint::<S>::as_public_input)
357:                 .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
358:             msm.scalars.clone(),
359:         ]
360:         .into_iter()
361:         .flatten()
362:         .collect::<Vec<_>>()
363:     }
364: 
365:     #[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
366:     fn from_public_input(_fields: &[S::F]) -> Option<Msm<S>> {
367:         unimplemented!("not invertible: the flat encoding loses structural metadata.")
368:     }
369: }
370: 
371: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedMsm<S> {
372:     /// Creates a new in-circuit MSM from parallel slices of scalars, bases,
373:     /// and labels.
374:     ///
375:     /// # Panics
376:     ///
377:     /// If `bases`, `scalars` and `labels` do not all have the same length.
378:     pub fn new(
379:         scalars: &[AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>],
380:         bases: &[AssignedPoint<S>],
381:         labels: &[PolynomialLabel],
382:     ) -> Self {
383:         assert_eq!(bases.len(), scalars.len());
384:         assert_eq!(bases.len(), labels.len());
385:         Self {
386:             bases: bases.to_vec(),
387:             scalars: scalars.to_vec(),
388:             labels: labels.to_vec(),
389:         }
390:     }
391: 
392:     /// The bases of this assigned MSM.
393:     pub fn bases(&self) -> Vec<AssignedPoint<S>> {
394:         self.bases.clone()
395:     }
396: 
397:     /// The scalars of this assigned MSM.
398:     pub fn scalars(&self) -> Vec<AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>> {
399:         self.scalars.clone()
400:     }
401: 
402:     /// The labels of this assigned MSM.
403:     pub fn labels(&self) -> Vec<PolynomialLabel> {
404:         self.labels.clone()
405:     }
406: 
407:     /// Creates a single-term MSM from the given scalar, base, and label.
408:     pub fn from_term(
409:         scalar: AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>,
410:         base: AssignedPoint<S>,
411:         label: PolynomialLabel,
412:     ) -> Self {
413:         Self::new(&[scalar], &[base], &[label])
414:     }
415: 
416:     /// Creates a single fixed-base term MSM with the given scalar and label.
417:     pub fn from_fixed_term(scalar: AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>, label: PolynomialLabel) -> Self {
418:         Self::from_term(scalar, AssignedPoint::Fixed, label)
419:     }
420: }
421: 
422: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedMsm<S> {
423:     /// Converts the off-circuit MSM into two vectors of scalars. The first
424:     /// will be used as a normal instance, whereas the second will be plugged-in
425:     /// in as a committed instance.
426:     ///
427:     /// The committed instance part corresponds to the scalars of the MSM.
428:     pub fn as_public_input_with_committed_scalars(msm: &Msm<S>) -> (Vec<S::F>, Vec<S::F>) {
429:         let normal_instance =
430:             msm.bases.iter().flat_map(AssignedPoint::<S>::as_public_input).collect();
431: 
432:         let committed_instance = msm.scalars.clone();
433: 
434:         (normal_instance, committed_instance)
435:     }
436: }
437: 
438: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedMsm<S> {
439:     pub(crate) fn in_circuit_as_public_input(
440:         &self,
441:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
442:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
443:     ) -> Result<Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>, Error> {
444:         Ok([
445:             self.bases
446:                 .iter()
447:                 .map(|base| base.in_circuit_as_public_input(layouter, curve_chip))
448:                 .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?
449:                 .concat(),
450:             self.scalars.iter().map(|s| s.clone().scalar).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
451:         ]
452:         .concat())
453:     }
454: 
455:     pub(crate) fn constrain_as_public_input(
456:         &self,
457:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
458:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
459:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
460:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
461:         self.bases
462:             .iter()
463:             .try_for_each(|base| base.constrain_as_public_input(layouter, curve_chip))?;
464: 
465:         self.scalars
466:             .iter()
467:             .try_for_each(|s| scalar_chip.constrain_as_public_input(layouter, &s.clone().scalar))
468:     }
469: 
470:     pub(crate) fn constrain_as_public_input_with_committed_scalars(
471:         &self,
472:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
473:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
474:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
475:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
476:         self.bases
477:             .iter()
478:             .try_for_each(|base| base.constrain_as_public_input(layouter, curve_chip))?;
479: 
480:         self.scalars.iter().try_for_each(|s| {
481:             let mut a = S::F::ZERO;
482:             s.scalar.clone().value().map(|v| a = *v);
483:             S::constrain_scalar_as_committed_public_input(layouter, scalar_chip, &s.scalar)
484:         })
485:     }
486: }
487: 
488: impl<S: SelfEmulation> AssignedMsm<S> {
489:     /// Witnesses all scalar cells and variable-base point cells for this MSM.
490:     ///
491:     /// The `labels` slice determines the shape: entries whose label is in
492:     /// `fixed_base_labels` are represented as `AssignedPoint::Fixed` (no cell
493:     /// allocated for the point); all other entries are assigned as
494:     /// `AssignedPoint::Variable`.
495:     ///
496:     /// # Warning
497:     ///
498:     /// Variable-base points are not checked for subgroup membership.
499:     ///
500:     /// # Errors
501:     ///
502:     /// If `msm_value` is known and its label list differs from `labels`.
503:     pub fn assign(
504:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
505:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
506:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
507:         labels: &[PolynomialLabel],
508:         fixed_base_labels: &HashSet<PolynomialLabel>,
509:         msm_value: Value<Msm<S>>,
510:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
511:         let len = labels.len();
512:         assert!(fixed_base_labels.len() <= len);
513:         msm_value.error_if_known_and(|msm| msm.labels != labels)?;
514: 
515:         let scalars_val = msm_value.as_ref().map(|msm| msm.scalars.clone()).transpose_vec(len);
516:         let bases_val = msm_value.as_ref().map(|msm| msm.bases.clone()).transpose_vec(len);
517: 
518:         let scalars = assign_bounded_scalars(layouter, scalar_chip, &scalars_val)?;
519:         let mut bases = Vec::<AssignedPoint<S>>::with_capacity(len);
520: 
521:         for i in 0..len {
522:             if fixed_base_labels.contains(&labels[i]) {
523:                 bases_val[i].error_if_known_and(|p| matches!(p, Point::Variable(..)))?;
524:                 bases.push(AssignedPoint::Fixed);
525:             } else {
526:                 bases_val[i].error_if_known_and(|p| matches!(p, Point::Fixed))?;
527:                 let p_val = bases_val[i].clone().map(|p| match p {
528:                     Point::Variable(p) => p,
529:                     Point::Fixed => unreachable!(),
530:                 });
531:                 let p = S::assign_without_subgroup_check(layouter, curve_chip, p_val)?;
532:                 bases.push(AssignedPoint::Variable(p));
533:             }
534:         }
535: 
536:         Ok(AssignedMsm {
537:             scalars,
538:             bases,
539:             labels: labels.to_vec(),
540:         })
541:     }
542: 
543:     /// An empty AssignedMsm with no fixed base scalars, that evaluates to the
544:     /// identity point.
545:     pub fn empty() -> Self {
546:         Self {
547:             scalars: vec![],
548:             bases: vec![],
549:             labels: vec![],
550:         }
551:     }
552: 
553:     /// Appends a `(scalar, base, label)` term to this MSM.
554:     pub fn add_term(
555:         &mut self,
556:         scalar: &AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>,
557:         base: &AssignedPoint<S>,
558:         label: &PolynomialLabel,
559:     ) {
560:         self.scalars.push(scalar.clone());
561:         self.bases.push(base.clone());
562:         self.labels.push(label.clone());
563:     }
564: 
565:     /// Adds two AssignedMsm.
566:     pub fn add_msm(&mut self, other: &Self) -> Result<(), Error> {
567:         self.scalars.extend(other.scalars.clone());
568:         self.bases.extend(other.bases.clone());
569:         self.labels.extend(other.labels.clone());
570:         Ok(())
571:     }
572: 
573:     /// In-circuit analog of [`Msm::collapse`].
574:     ///
575:     /// Reduces the variable-base part to a single MSM point via the circuit's
576:     /// MSM chip, labeling the result with `label`. Fixed-base terms sharing the
577:     /// same label have their scalars added in-circuit. After the call, the MSM
578:     /// has one `Variable` entry (the collapsed result) and one `Fixed` entry
579:     /// per distinct fixed label.
580:     ///
581:     /// This function mutates `self`.
582:     pub fn collapse(
583:         &mut self,
584:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
585:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
586:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
587:         label: PolynomialLabel,
588:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
589:         // We allocate max capacity but we may not fill it.
590:         let n = self.bases.len();
591:         let mut variable_bases = Vec::with_capacity(n);
592:         let mut variable_scalars = Vec::with_capacity(n);
593: 
594:         let mut fixed_base_scalars =
595:             BTreeMap::<PolynomialLabel, AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>>::new();
596: 
597:         for i in 0..n {
598:             match self.bases[i].clone() {
599:                 AssignedPoint::Variable(p) => {
600:                     let s = self.scalars[i].clone();
601:                     variable_bases.push(p.clone());
602:                     variable_scalars.push((s.scalar, s.bound.bits() as usize));
603:                 }
604:                 AssignedPoint::Fixed => match fixed_base_scalars.entry(self.labels[i].clone()) {
605:                     Entry::Occupied(mut occ) => {
606:                         let s = add_bounded_scalars(
607:                             layouter,
608:                             scalar_chip,
609:                             occ.get(),
610:                             &self.scalars[i],
611:                         )?;
612:                         *occ.get_mut() = s;
613:                     }
614:                     Entry::Vacant(vac) => {
615:                         vac.insert(self.scalars[i].clone());
616:                     }
617:                 },
618:             }
619:         }
620: 
621:         let mut bases = vec![AssignedPoint::Fixed; fixed_base_scalars.len()];
622:         let mut scalars: Vec<_> = fixed_base_scalars.values().cloned().collect();
623:         let mut labels: Vec<_> = fixed_base_scalars.keys().cloned().collect();
624: 
625:         let collapsed_base = S::msm(layouter, curve_chip, &variable_scalars, &variable_bases)?;
626:         bases.push(AssignedPoint::Variable(collapsed_base));
627:         scalars.push(AssignedBoundedScalar::one(layouter, scalar_chip)?);
628:         labels.push(label);
629: 
630:         self.bases = bases;
631:         self.scalars = scalars;
632:         self.labels = labels;
633: 
634:         Ok(())
635:     }
636: 
637:     /// Resolves all `Fixed` bases by substituting their assigned circuit
638:     /// points.
639:     ///
640:     /// Each `Fixed` entry is replaced with `Variable(fixed_bases[label])`.
641:     /// After this call, no `Fixed` entries remain.
642:     ///
643:     /// This is the in-circuit analog of [Msm::resolve_fixed_bases].
644:     ///
645:     /// # Panics
646:     ///
647:     /// If any `Fixed` label is absent from `fixed_bases`.
648:     pub fn resolve_fixed_bases(
649:         &mut self,
650:         fixed_bases: &BTreeMap<PolynomialLabel, S::AssignedPoint>,
651:     ) {
652:         for (i, base) in self.bases.iter_mut().enumerate() {
653:             match base {
654:                 AssignedPoint::Variable(..) => (),
655:                 AssignedPoint::Fixed => {
656:                     let l = &self.labels[i];
657:                     let p = fixed_bases.get(l).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("Base not provided: {l}"));
658:                     *base = AssignedPoint::Variable(p.clone())
659:                 }
660:             }
661:         }
662:     }
663: 
664:     /// Scales all the scalars of the AssignedMsm by the given factor r.
665:     ///
666:     /// This function mutates self.
667:     pub fn scale(
668:         &mut self,
669:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
670:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
671:         r: &AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>,
672:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
673:         self.scalars = (self.scalars.iter())
674:             .map(|s| mul_bounded_scalars(layouter, scalar_chip, s, r))
675:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, Error>>()?;
676: 
677:         Ok(())
678:     }
679: 
680:     /// Accumulates two AssignedMSMs with the a given scalar r.
681:     /// The resulting AssignedMSMs evaluates (on `fixed_bases`) to
682:     /// `self.eval(fixed_bases) + r * other.eval(fixed_bases)`.
683:     pub fn accumulate_with_r(
684:         &self,
685:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
686:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
687:         other: &Self,
688:         r: &AssignedBoundedScalar<S::F>,
689:     ) -> Result<Self, Error> {
690:         let mut other = other.clone();
691:         other.scale(layouter, scalar_chip, r)?;
692: 
693:         let mut acc = self.clone();
694:         acc.add_msm(&other)?;
695: 
696:         Ok(acc)
697:     }
698: }

14: //! A module for the in-circuit permutation argument. It is the in-circuit
15: //! analog of file proofs/src/plonk/permutation/verifier.rs.
16: //!
17: //! The "expressions" part is dealt with in our `expressions/` directory.
18: 
19: use midnight_proofs::{
20:     circuit::Layouter,
21:     plonk::{ConstraintSystem, Error},
22:     poly::PolynomialLabel,
23: };
24: 
25: use crate::{
26:     field::AssignedNative,
27:     verifier::{
28:         SelfEmulation,
29:         pcs::{InCircuitPCS, VerifierQuery},
30:         transcript_gadget::TranscriptGadget,
31:     },
32: };

34: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
35: pub(crate) struct Committed<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
36:     permutation_product_commitments: Vec<PCS::AssignedCommitment>,
37: }
38: 
39: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
40: pub(crate) struct EvaluatedSet<S: SelfEmulation> {
41:     pub(crate) permutation_product_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
42:     pub(crate) permutation_product_next_eval: AssignedNative<S::F>,
43:     pub(crate) permutation_product_last_eval: Option<AssignedNative<S::F>>,
44: }
45: 
46: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
47: pub(crate) struct CommonEvaluated<S: SelfEmulation> {
48:     pub(crate) permutation_evals: Vec<AssignedNative<S::F>>,
49: }
50: 
51: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
52: pub(crate) struct Evaluated<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> {
53:     coms: Committed<S, PCS>,
54:     pub(crate) sets: Vec<EvaluatedSet<S>>,
55: }
56: 
57: pub(crate) fn read_product_commitments<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>>(
58:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
59:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
60:     cs: &ConstraintSystem<S::F>,
61: ) -> Result<Committed<S, PCS>, Error> {
62:     let chunk_len = cs.degree() - 2;
63: 
64:     let nb_chunks = cs.permutation().get_columns().len().div_ceil(chunk_len);
65: 
66:     let permutation_product_commitments = (0..nb_chunks)
67:         .map(|i| {
68:             PCS::read_commitment(
69:                 transcript_gadget,
70:                 layouter,
71:                 &[PolynomialLabel::PermutationAccumulator(i)],
72:             )
73:         })
74:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
75: 
76:     Ok(Committed {
77:         permutation_product_commitments,
78:     })
79: }
80: 
81: /// This is the in-circuit analog of `evaluate` implemented for `VerifyingKey`
82: /// in halo2 src/plonk/permutation/verifier.rs.
83: ///
84: /// Instead of evaluating it for the `VerifyingKey`, we directly require the
85: /// `nb_perm_commitments` as an argument.
86: pub(crate) fn evaluate_permutation_common<S: SelfEmulation>(
87:     layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
88:     transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
89:     nb_perm_commitments: usize,
90: ) -> Result<CommonEvaluated<S>, Error> {
91:     let permutation_evals = (0..nb_perm_commitments)
92:         .map(|_| transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter))
93:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
94: 
95:     Ok(CommonEvaluated { permutation_evals })
96: }
97: 
98: impl<S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> Committed<S, PCS> {
99:     pub(crate) fn evaluate(
100:         self,
101:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
102:         transcript_gadget: &mut TranscriptGadget<S>,
103:     ) -> Result<Evaluated<S, PCS>, Error> {
104:         let mut sets = vec![];
105: 
106:         let mut iter = self.permutation_product_commitments.iter();
107: 
108:         while iter.next().is_some() {
109:             let permutation_product_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
110:             let permutation_product_next_eval = transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?;
111:             let permutation_product_last_eval = if iter.len() > 0 {
112:                 Some(transcript_gadget.read_scalar(layouter)?)
113:             } else {
114:                 None
115:             };
116: 
117:             sets.push(EvaluatedSet {
118:                 permutation_product_eval,
119:                 permutation_product_next_eval,
120:                 permutation_product_last_eval,
121:             });
122:         }
123: 
124:         Ok(Evaluated { coms: self, sets })
125:     }
126: }
127: 
128: // "expressions" is implemented in `expressions/permutation.rs`
129: 
130: impl<'a, S: SelfEmulation, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>> Evaluated<S, PCS> {
131:     pub(crate) fn queries(
132:         &'a self,
133:         x: &AssignedNative<S::F>,      // evaluation point x
134:         x_next: &AssignedNative<S::F>, // x * \omega
135:         x_last: &AssignedNative<S::F>, // x * \omega^(-blinding_factors + 1)
136:     ) -> Vec<VerifierQuery<'a, S, PCS>> {
137:         let mut queries = vec![];
138:         for (i, set) in self.sets.iter().enumerate() {
139:             // Open permutation product commitments at x and \omega x
140:             queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
141:                 x,
142:                 &self.coms.permutation_product_commitments[i],
143:                 PolynomialLabel::PermutationAccumulator(i),
144:                 &set.permutation_product_eval,
145:             ));
146:             queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
147:                 x_next,
148:                 &self.coms.permutation_product_commitments[i],
149:                 PolynomialLabel::PermutationAccumulator(i),
150:                 &set.permutation_product_next_eval,
151:             ));
152:         }
153: 
154:         // Open it at \omega^{last} x for all but the last set
155:         for (i, set) in self.sets.iter().enumerate().rev().skip(1) {
156:             queries.push(VerifierQuery::new(
157:                 x_last,
158:                 &self.coms.permutation_product_commitments[i],
159:                 PolynomialLabel::PermutationAccumulator(i),
160:                 set.permutation_product_last_eval.as_ref().unwrap(),
161:             ));
162:         }
163: 
164:         queries
165:     }
166: }
167: 
168: impl<S: SelfEmulation> CommonEvaluated<S> {
169:     pub(crate) fn queries<'a, PCS: InCircuitPCS<S>>(
170:         &self,
171:         vkey_commitments: &[&'a PCS::AssignedCommitment],
172:         x: &AssignedNative<S::F>, // evaluation point x
173:     ) -> Vec<VerifierQuery<'a, S, PCS>> {
174:         assert_eq!(vkey_commitments.len(), self.permutation_evals.len());
175: 
176:         vkey_commitments
177:             .iter()
178:             .zip(self.permutation_evals.iter())
179:             .enumerate()
180:             .map(|(i, (commitment, eval))| {
181:                 VerifierQuery::new(x, *commitment, PolynomialLabel::PermutationFixed(i), eval)
182:             })
183:             .collect()
184:     }
185: }

41: /// Gadget used to run the transcript reader in-circuit.
42: #[derive(Clone, Debug)]
43: pub struct TranscriptGadget<S: SelfEmulation> {
44:     scalar_chip: S::ScalarChip,
45:     curve_chip: S::CurveChip,
46:     sponge_chip: S::SpongeChip,
47:     sponge_state: Option<SpongeState<S>>,
48:     // Track the number of field elements we have in the buffer.
49:     input_len: usize,
50:     // Transcript reader is included, to help parse the proof. This parsing
51:     // *does not* need to be verified in-circuit.
52:     transcript_reader: Option<CircuitTranscript<S::Hash>>,
53: }
54: 
55: impl<S: SelfEmulation> TranscriptGadget<S> {
56:     /// Creates a new `TranscriptGadget` from the corresponding chips.
57:     pub fn new(
58:         scalar_chip: &S::ScalarChip,
59:         curve_chip: &S::CurveChip,
60:         sponge_chip: &S::SpongeChip,
61:     ) -> Self {
62:         Self {
63:             scalar_chip: scalar_chip.clone(),
64:             curve_chip: curve_chip.clone(),
65:             sponge_chip: sponge_chip.clone(),
66:             sponge_state: None,
67:             input_len: 0,
68:             transcript_reader: None,
69:         }
70:     }
71: 
72:     /// Initialises the `TranscriptGadget`, by initialising the sponge buffer,
73:     /// from a given witnessed proof in the form of `Value<Vec<u8>>`.
74:     pub fn init_with_proof(
75:         &mut self,
76:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
77:         proof: Value<Vec<u8>>,
78:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
79:         self.sponge_state = Some(self.sponge_chip.init(layouter, None)?);
80: 
81:         // Unwrapping the witness. The amount of points read from the proof is
82:         // fixed for a given `Architecture`, and does not depend on the size of
83:         // the proof.
84:         // The caveat with this approach is that our in-circuit verifier will not
85:         // be able to verify that the proof did not include extra bytes after
86:         // all the relevant bytes have been read. This is not an issue anyway.
87:         let mut proof_bytes = Vec::new();
88:         proof.clone().map(|pi| proof_bytes.extend_from_slice(&pi));
89:         self.transcript_reader = Some(CircuitTranscript::init_from_bytes(&proof_bytes));
90: 
91:         Ok(())
92:     }
93: 
94:     /// Absorbs a scalar into the transcript.
95:     pub fn common_scalar(
96:         &mut self,
97:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
98:         scalar: &AssignedNative<S::F>,
99:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
100:         self.input_len += 1;
101:         let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
102:         self.sponge_chip.absorb(layouter, state, std::slice::from_ref(scalar))
103:     }
104: 
105:     /// Absorbs a commitment into the transcript, one inner polynomial point at
106:     /// a time, matching the off-circuit `Hashable::to_input`.
107:     pub fn common_commitment(
108:         &mut self,
109:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
110:         commitment: &AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S>,
111:     ) -> Result<(), Error> {
112:         for inner in commitment.0.iter() {
113:             let pis = match inner {
114:                 AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(AssignedPoint::Variable(p), _label) => {
115:                     self.curve_chip.as_public_input(layouter, p)
116:                 }
117:                 AssignedKZGCommitment::Simple(AssignedPoint::Fixed, label) => Err(Synthesis(
118:                     format!("Fixed commitments cannot be added to the transcript: {label}"),
119:                 )),
120:                 AssignedKZGCommitment::Linear(_, _, labels) => Err(Synthesis(format!(
121:                     "Linear commitments cannot be added to the transcript: {labels:?}"
122:                 ))),
123:             }?;
124: 
125:             self.input_len += pis.len();
126: 
127:             let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
128:             self.sponge_chip.absorb(layouter, state, &pis)?;
129:         }
130:         Ok(())
131:     }
132: 
133:     /// Derives a scalar challenge from the current transcript.
134:     pub fn squeeze_challenge(
135:         &mut self,
136:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
137:     ) -> Result<AssignedNative<S::F>, Error> {
138:         let state = self.sponge_state.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
139:         self.sponge_chip.squeeze(layouter, state)
140:     }
141: 
142:     /// Reads `labels.len()` curve points from the prover transcript (one per
143:     /// polynomial held by the commitment), absorbs them into the running hash
144:     /// state, and tags each polynomial with its label.
145:     ///
146:     /// # Warning
147:     ///
148:     /// The received points are not enforced to be in the prime-order subgroup.
149:     pub fn read_commitment(
150:         &mut self,
151:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
152:         labels: &[PolynomialLabel],
153:     ) -> Result<AssignedKZGMultiCommitment<S>, Error> {
154:         let mut inners = Vec::with_capacity(labels.len());
155:         for label in labels {
156:             let reader =
157:                 self.transcript_reader.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
158:             // If an error, do not fail, assign a default commitment instead.
159:             // (This allows us to parse dummy proofs.)
160:             let point: Value<S::C> = match reader.read::<S::C>() {
161:                 Ok(point) => Value::known(point),
162:                 Err(_) => Value::known(S::C::default()),
163:             };
164:             let assigned_point =
165:                 S::assign_without_subgroup_check(layouter, &self.curve_chip, point)?;
166:             inners.push(AssignedKZGCommitment::simple(assigned_point, label.clone()));
167:         }
168: 
169:         let assigned_com = AssignedKZGMultiCommitment(inners);
170:         self.common_commitment(layouter, &assigned_com)?;
171: 
172:         Ok(assigned_com)
173:     }
174: 
175:     /// Reads a scalar from the reader buffer, and adds it to the transcript.
176:     /// Think of the read scalar as a witness freely chosen by the prover.
177:     pub fn read_scalar(
178:         &mut self,
179:         layouter: &mut impl Layouter<S::F>,
180:     ) -> Result<AssignedNative<S::F>, Error> {
181:         let reader = self.transcript_reader.as_mut().expect("You must init the transcript gadget");
182:         // If an error, do not fail, assign a default scalar instead.
183:         // (This allows us to parse dummy proofs.)
184:         let scalar: Value<S::F> = match reader.read::<S::F>() {
185:             Ok(scalar) => Value::known(scalar),
186:             Err(_) => Value::known(S::F::ZERO),
187:         };
188: 
189:         let assigned_scalar = self.scalar_chip.assign(layouter, scalar)?;
190:         self.common_scalar(layouter, &assigned_scalar)?;
191: 
192:         Ok(assigned_scalar)
193:     }
194: }
